sanātana-gosvāmī (dig-darṣinī) : [bṛ.bhā. 2.7.105] evaṁ stuti-prabhāvāt bhāsita-bhagavat-prasādatas tadānīm evākhilābhimānāpagamena jātaṁ parama-dainyam āśritaḥ san dīrgha-kālaṁ vraja-vāsi-samīpe'vasthātum ayogyam ātmānaṁ manyamāno'parādhāntaram api āśaṅkyamāno nija-sthānaṁ gantuṁ prārthayate—anujānīhi [bhā.pu. 10.14.39] iti |

nija-mahimānaṁ mādṛśāṁ ca nikṛṣṭatvādikaṁ sarvaṁ tvam eva vetsi, yataḥ sarva-dṛk sarvam eva sākṣāt sadā paśyasi | atas tvāṁ stotum api nārhāmīti māṁ prati gamanāyānujñāṁ dehi |

yad vā, nanu, mama rūpa-nāmādīnāṁ bhakter bhaktānām eṣāṁ ca vraja-vāsināṁ māhātmyaṁ viśeṣato varṇaya ? tatrāha—sarvam iti | vaibhavam ity anuvartata eva | tat tad aśeṣaṁ māhātmyaṁ sarva-dṛg api tvaṁ vetsi | kiṁ kākūktyā, naiva jānāsīty arthaḥ, tat-tan-mahimno'ntābhāvāt |

athavā, bho brahman ! tvayātra kiñcij janmādhunaiva prārthitaṁ | tat katham anyatra gantum icchasi ? mat-karṇa-sukhāvaham īdṛśaṁ vraja-mahima-varṇanam evātrāvasthāya vidhehi | tatrāha—sarvam iti |

ayam arthaḥ—caturmukha-śarīreṇānena sukham atra vastuṁ nārhāmi iti śarīrāntaraṁ ca mama dviparārdhābhyantare durghaṭam | ato'dhunā vraja-vāsinām eṣāṁ pāda-rajo'bhiṣeko'pi mama durlabha eva bhavitā | kiṁ ca, nijāśakye eṣāṁ mahima-varṇane pravṛttiḥ parama-lajjā-hetur doṣāyaivety-ādikaṁ madīyam | tvam api ṛṇīva sadā vraja-vāsi-vaśya-tadīya-prema-bhakty-eka-raso'taḥ kṣaṇam api taiḥ sahāsaṅkocaṁ sukha-vihāraṁ vinānyatra kim api na rocate ity-ādikam ātmanaś ca sarvaṁ vṛttam | kṛṣṇa ! he vraja-janaikānanda ! tvaṁ vetsy eva tat katham atra sthāsyāmi ? iti |

nanu, sarvajñena bhavatā pūrvaṁ katham evam aparāmṛśya tathā yācitam ? tad-varṇane vā pravṛttaṁ ca ? tatrāha—sarvaṁ paśyati jānātīti sarva-dṛk tvam eva, mādṛśās tu kiñcij jñā eva | yad vā, sarvaṁ darśayati jñānādi-śakti-pradānena sarvatra sarvaṁ pravartayatīti sarva-dṛk | yathā tvayāntaryāmiṇā preritaṁ, tathaiva mayā prārthitaṁ pravṛttaṁ ca | tatra mama na ko'pi guṇo doṣo vā | guṇaś cet prerakasyeśvarasyaiva, sa-doṣaś cet tena kila prerye'rpayituṁ na yujyata iti bhāvaḥ | etad api sarvaṁ vetsi |

nanu, dāsye saty evam uktaṁ syāt | tac ca jagat-svāminas tava na sambhavaty eva ? tatrāha—tvam iti | tvam eva nāthaḥ, na tv aham anyo vā kaścit | tatra ca jagatām eva nāthaḥ | ato mamāpi jagannāthatvāt tava dāsa evāham iti bhāvaḥ |

nanu, sṛṣṭi-kartuḥ pitāmahasya tava putra-pautrādi-rūpaṁ jagat-trayādhikriyamāṇam etat pratyakṣaṁ dṛśyate | kathaṁ me jagannāthatā yayā tvaṁ mad-dāsaḥ syāḥ ? tatrāha—jagad iti | sarvam evaitat tava, tvayi arpitam āhitam, ata eva tvad-adhīnam ity arthaḥ |

yad vā, tavaitad akhilaṁ tvayaiva mayy arpitaṁ sthāpitaṁ, tvat-kartṛkārpaṇa-pāṭavenaiva madīyatvena parisphuratīti bhāvaḥ | athavā, mama jagad-īśatve jagatām eveśitavyatayā dāsatā sahajā vartata eva | tat kathaṁ parato ghoṣaṇayā ? pūrvam abhaktās tvayā ninditāḥ, kathaṁ vā bhaktim atīva praśaśya paramautsukyena svayaṁ dāsyaṁ ? tatrāpy atra vraje kim api janma prārthitam ? satyaṁ tvad-adhīnatayā svābhāvike tvad-dāsye vartamāne'pi premṇā vicitra-bhaktyaiva dāsatā paramottamā sudurlabhā sarvair api sādhyā | sā ca sarvārpaṇaṁ vinā yathārthaṁ na sidhyatīti tad-arthaṁ sarvam evārpayāmīty āha—mamatāspadaṁ jagad-ahantāspadaṁ caitac charīraṁ stotrādikam api mayā tvayy evārpitam |

etac ca dhvanitam—yadyapi jagat sarvaṁ tava jagannāthasyaiva, tathāpi he kṛṣṇa ! jagannāthatvād bahir-antar-īśvarasyāntaryāminas tava preraṇayā tvadīya-mahima-viśeṣālocanena paramottama-dāsya-prāptaye tvayy eva mayā piṣṭa-peṣaṇa-nyāyenāpy arpitam | evam anadhikāriṇo mama tādṛśa-durghaṭa-dravya-prārthanā-doṣo'pi tvayā kṣantavya eveti ||105||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : itthaṁ stuti-prabhāvajanita-śrī-bhagavat-prasāda-viśeṣato’khilābhimānāpagamena parama-dainyam āśrito’taj-jñānādin eva nija-sthānaṁ gantum anujñāṁ prārthayate—anujānīhīti | kṛṣṇa ! he nijāśeṣa-bhagavatā prakaṭanapara ! sarvam uktatvad vaibhavaṁ tvam eva vetsi, yataḥ sarva-dṛk sarvajñaḥ | yad vā, sarva-dṛg api tvaṁ vetsi kim ?—kākvā, naivety artho’nantatvāt, atas tat stotum api nārhāmīti prasthāpayety arthaḥ | kim ca, tvad bhakti-siddhaye’bhimāna-tyāgena nijaṁ sarvaṁ tvay arpayāmīty āha—tvam eveti | anyat tair vyañjitam |

athavā, bho brāhman ! tvayātra kim api janmādhunaiva prārthitam, tat kim anyatra gantum icchasi ? tatrāha—sarvam iti | caturmukhenānena deva-dehenātra sthātuṁ nārhāmīty ādikaṁ mad-vṛttam, tvaṁ caiṣāṁ premavaśyo yatheṣṭam etān ramayituṁ vyagro’sīty ādikaṁ ca vṛttam, he kṛṣṇa ! vraja-janaika-priya ! tvaṁ vetsyeva, tat katham atra sthāsysāmīti bhāvaḥ | kintu nija-tat-prārthita-siddhaye sarvaṁ tvayy arpayāmīty āha—jagatāṁ nāthas tvam evety arpaṇābhimāno’pi na mama ghaṭate’to’rpaṇam apy arpitam iti bhāvaḥ | athavā bhoḥ parameṣṭhin ! sṛṣṭhādinā tāvaj jagadīśas tvam, tat katham īdṛśaṁ dainyam ālambase ? tatrāha—tavaivaitaj jagad arpitaṁ mayi tvayaivāto’tra na mama nāthatvam, kintu tvat-kartṛkārpaṇa-mahimnaiva mad adhikṛtatayā sphuratīti bhāvaḥ ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : atha tat tat prakaraṇante yā daityātmikā bhaktir eva tat prāpti-kāraṇatvena darśitā, tām evāviṣkurvan anujñāṁ prārthayate—he kṛṣṇa ! sarvendriyākarṣaka-rūpa-guṇa ! anujānīhi anena gamanānujñeyaṁ nātmecchayā prārthyate kiṁ tvatrāvasthānānarhatayaiveti sā ca prārthitasyāpi tasya maunitayeti bhāvaḥ | tvayi ca yan mayoktaṁ yad vā vaktavyaṁ tat sarvaṁ punar uktam evety āha—sarvaṁ sva-vaibhavaṁ mad vaibhavaṁ ca svamano-gataṁ man mano-gataṁ ca sva-nigūḍha-līlatvaṁ mad ayogyatvaṁ cety ādikaṁ tvam eva vetsi nāham ity atas tvad ājñāṁ vinā tṛṇādi-rūpeṇāpy eṣāṁ tṛṇādīnāṁ saubhāgyam āsādayituṁ na śaknomiti bhāvaḥ | viṣayam api tyaktuṁ na śaknomīty āha—tvam eveti | tava tvayaiva mahyam arpitaṁ dāsasyājñāsthāyitvam eva yuktam iti bhāvaḥ | yadi caivaṁ brūṣe apahṛtātmanāṁ bāla-vatsānāṁ darśanaṁ vinā katham anujñāṁ yācase tatrāpy evaṁ nivedayāmīty āha-sarvaṁ tvaṁ vetsi sarva-dṛg iti cātra jñāpitam ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : sarvam iti tathāpy atrāvasthānājñāyā alābhāt kathaṁ sthāsyāmīti bhāvaḥ | viṣayam api na tyaktuṁ śaknomīty āha—taveti | tava tvayaiva mayy arpitaṁ dāsasyājñāsthāyitvam eva ca yuktam iti bhāvaḥ ||39.40||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : upasaṁharati—anujānīhīty-ādi | he kṛṣṇa ! mām anujānīhi | nanu kim anujñāsyāmīty-āśaṅkyāha—sarvaṁ tvaṁ vetsi, tat kiṁ kathanīyam | nanu katham ahaṁ vedmi? tatrāha—sarva-dṛk sarvajño'si | nanu jñātaṁ bho jñātam, tat bhūri-bhāgya miheti yad uktam tad eva tava tu jagat-sraṣṭur evaṁ na yujyata ity-āśaṅkyāha—tvam eveti jagatāṁ nāthas tvam eva, nānyo’smad-vidhādiḥ | kutaḥ ? ity āha—jagac caitat tavārpitaṁ tvayy evārpitam | tvayy arpitasya nāthas tvāṁ vinā ko’nyo bhavatu ? tasmād yathābhilaṣitam eva bhūyād ity anujānīhi ||39 ||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : nanu, mama vaibhavaṁ tava māstu gocaras tava vaibhavam ahaṁ vedmi na vettīti vakroktim āśaṅkaya kim aham atra pratyuttaraṁ kuryyāmiti vyañjayan sa-lajjaṁ sa-nirvedam āha—anujānīhīti | antarbhāvitaṇy artham anu anujñāpayety arthaḥ | atra sthale kṣaṇam api sthātum ayogyam atinīcaṁ māmājñāpaya yādṛśo'haṁ tādṛśaṁ sthalaṁ satya-lokam eva gaccheyamiti bhāvaḥ | he kṛṣṇeti citta tū tvam atrākarṣaray eva kiṁ tu tad bhūri-bhāgyam iha janmeti mat-prārthanāyāṁ dṛg-iṅgitenāpy astviti śrī-caraṇair noktam ataḥ kiṁ kurve tasmāt tvat-pulina-bhojana-velāvantarāyaṁ kurvann ayam aparādhī tval-līlā-prātikūlyād eva śrī-mukhodgata-vacana-sudhā-leśam apy anāpnuvann aham ito jhaṭity eva dūram apasarāmi tvaṁ vatsān kālayitvā puline bhujānaḥ priya-sakhaiḥ saha sahāsokti-pratyukti-kautukaṁ bhojana-līlāśeṣa samāpayeti dhvanayaḥ | ayam ahaṁ tvatitāralyāt punaḥ punaḥ kiṁ vā, vijñāpayāmīty āha, sarvamīty āha, sarvam asmad-ādīnāṁ mano-vapur-vācāṁ vaibhavaṁ tvam eva vetsi kiṁ ca nāham asya jagataḥ spṛṣṭatvān nāthaḥ kiṁ tu tvam eva jagatām anyeṣām api bahūnāṁ nāthaḥ ata etac ca jagat kṣudrataraṁ tava tvadīyam eva tvayy arpitaṁ yamicchasi yogyamasya jānāsi tam asyādhikārikaṁ kurviti bhāvaḥ ||39||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : na vyākhyātam.

...

|| 10.14.40 ||

śrī-kṛṣṇa vṛṣṇi-kula-puṣkara-joṣa-dāyin |

kṣmā-nirjara-dvija-paśūdadhi-vṛddhi-kārin ||

uddharma-śārvara-hara kṣitirākṣasa-dhrug |

ākalpam ārkam arhan bhagavan namas te ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tad evaṁ kiṅkaraṁ māṁ prasthāpayety aty-ādareṇa namas-karoti, śrī-kṛṣṇeti | vṛṣṇi-kula-padmasya prīti-dātṛtvena he sūryopama | kṣmā cāvanir nirjarā devāś ca dvijāś ca paśavaś ca ta evodadhayas teṣāṁ vṛddhi-kāritvena, he candropama ! uddharmaḥ pākhaṇḍa-dharmas tad eva, śārvaraṁ tamas tad dharatīti tathā, anena dvayopama, he kṣiti-rākṣasa-dhruk kṣitau rākṣasāḥ kaṁsādayaḥ udyann eva tebhyo druhyatīti punaḥ sūryopama | sūryādy-upamāpi nyūneti matvāha—ārkam arkam abhivyāpya sarveṣām arhan pūjya bhagavan, ākalpaṁ kalpa-paryantaṁ te tubhyaṁ nama iti ||40||

sa-sakhi-vatsaka-mokṣaṇa-kautuka-

druta-vilambita-putra-mude’dbhutam |

nikhila-rūpam ato dadhad acyuto’

vatu sa vaḥ kalayan vraja-maṅgalam ||*||

————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tad evam ity uktārtham | paśavo gāvaś ca | udgato dharmo yatra sa uddharmaḥ | śarvaryāṁ bhavaṁ śārvaram | yady api mām aparādhinaṁ vijñāya na brūṣe tad api svanetrābhyāṁ sānugrahāvalokanāmṛtaṁ tu mahyaṁ dehi, yathā tenaivāhāreṇa nityaṁ prāṇān rakṣan kalpa-paryantaṁ jīvituṁ prabhaviṣyāmīti vyañjayan praṇam iti—śrī-kṛṣṇeti | sūrya-svarūpaṁ dakṣiṇaṁ netram ālakṣy āha—vṛṣṇi-klula-padmasya poṣaṁ praphullatvaṁ tat pradāyin | mām api padmasantānaṁ kṛpayā praphullayeti bhāvaḥ | candra-svarūpaṁ vāmaṁ netram

ālakṣyāha—kṣmā tatratyā manuṣyādayaḥ, nirjarāḥ svargasthā devāḥ, dvijāḥ paśavaś ca vṛndāvanasthāḥ, paśavo gāvaś ca ta evoddhyas teṣāṁ vṛddhi-kārin | mām api devādhamaṁ kṛpayā vardhayeti bhāvaḥ | yugapad eva netre dve eva puṣpavantāvālakṣyāha—uddharmaḥ, pāṣaṇḍa-dharmaḥ sa eva śārvaram andhatamasam śārvaraṁ tv andhatamase ity amaraḥ | tad dharatīti tathā | tena sva-prabhau tvayy api māyā-cikīrṣā-lakṣaṇaṁ pāṣaṇḍaṁ mama hara kṛpayā, yathā punar evaṁ na kuryām iti bhāvaḥ | kṣiti-rākṣasāḥ aghāsurādayas tebhyo druhyati droheṇāpi svargatiṁ dadāsīty atas tvad vasyasya vṛndavatsa-vṛndadrohitvāvatsa-loka-brahma-rākṣasaṁ mām api daṇḍa-pradānenāpi saṁskuruṣveti bhāvaḥ | sva-prabhor anugrahaṁ nigrahaṁ vā dṛṣṭvā dāso jīvitum utsahate, audāsīnyaṁ dṛṣṭvā tu na prāṇān dhartum īṣṭa iti bhāvaḥ || atra viśvanāthaḥ

toṣaṇīkārās tu—prīti-sādara-sarva-sampadanubhava-pūrvakaṁ praṇāmāya prathamataḥ kṛṣṇeti-svarūpa-nāmnā sambodhanam ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : naumīty upakramya namaskāreṇopasaṁharati—śrī-kṛṣṇa ity-ādi ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (dig-darṣinī) : [bṛ.bhā. 2.7.106] tad evaṁ kiṅkaraṁ prasthāpayety ādareṇa namaskaroti—śrī-kṛṣṇeti [bhā.pu. 10.14.40] | vṛṣṇi-kula-padmasya prīti-dātṛtvena he sūryopama ! kṣmā ca nirjarāś ca devāḥ, dvijāś ca, paśavaś ca, te eva udadhayaḥ, teṣāṁ vṛddhi-kāritvena he candropama ! uddharmaḥ pāṣaṇḍa-dharmaḥ, tad eva śārvaraṁ tamaḥ uddharatīti tathā | anena dvayopamaḥ | he kṣiti-rākṣasa-dhruk ! kṣitau rākṣasāḥ kaṁsādayaḥ udyann eva tebhyo druhyatīti punaḥ sūryopamā | sūryādy-upamāpi nyūneti matvāha—ārka arkam abhivyāpya sarveṣām arhaṁ pūjya bhagavan ākalpaṁ kalpa-paryantaṁ te tubhyaṁ namaḥ |

athavā itthaṁ sañjāta-parama-bhaktyā vicitra-nāma-saṅkīrtana-rūpāṁ paramāṁ stutim ante kurvan prārabdhaṁ samāpayituṁ praṇaman sarvam avatāra-prayojanam ekenaiva ślokena saṁkṣipya varṇayati—śrī-kṛṣṇeti | tatra prathamaṁ vṛṣṇi-kuleti | sūryodayena kamalānāṁ tamo-nirasana-prakāśanādikam ivāvirbhāva-mātreṇa śrī-vasudevādika-yādava-vargasya duḥkha-nāśa-pūrvakam ānanda-dātṛtvaṁ varṇitam | tathā ca daśama-skandhe—

sa bibhrat pauruṣaṁ dhāma rājamāno yathā raviḥ |

durāsado durviṣaho bhūtānāṁ sambabhūva ha ||[bhā.pu. 10.2.17] iti |

sa ānakadundubhir vasudevaḥ | tathā deva-stutau—diṣṭyā hare'syā bhavataḥ pado bhuvo bhāropanītas tava janmaneśituḥ [bhā.pu. 10.2.38] ity-ādi |

diṣṭyāmba te kukṣi-gataḥ paraḥ pumān

aṁśena sākṣād-bhagavān bhavāya naḥ |

mā bhūd bhayaṁ bhojapater mumūrṣor

goptā yadūnāṁ bhavitā tavātmajaḥ ||[bhā.pu. 10.2.41] iti |

tato janmādhyāye—mahī maṅgala-bhūyiṣṭha-pura-grāma-vrajākarā [bhā.pu. 10.3.2] ity-ādy-uktānusāreṇa pṛthivyā devānāṁ brāhmaṇānāṁ ca tathā paśūdadhir nandasya vrajas tasya ceti gavādīnām api vardhanam uktam | yadyapi pṛthivyā vardhanenaiva tad-antar-gatānāṁ yādava-dvija-vrajānāṁ vṛddhi-kāritvam āyāti, tathāpi bhakta-vātsalyaṁ go-brāhmaṇa-hita-kāritvaṁ ca sarvatraiva bhagavan-māhātmyam avaśyaṁ varṇyata ity ato'thavāvatāre'smin viśeṣataḥ eṣāṁ saṁvardhanam ity ataḥ pṛthag grahaṇam |

adhunā nija-prema-bhakti-vistārakatvam evāha—uddharmaḥ ucco dharmaḥ avaśya-pratipālya-svadharmādi-lakṣaṇaḥ, taṁ śarvarī-sambandhi-tamo-bhayādikaṁ ca | gopīnāṁ rāsa-krīḍayā haratīti | tathā abhūtam api etad avaśyam-bhāvitvena sarvajñatvāt sūcitam | yad vā, dharmo mad-bhakti-kṛt proktaḥ [bhā.pu. 11.19.27] ity ekādaśa-skandhe bhagavad-vacanād dharmo'tra bhakti-lakṣaṇaḥ tad-viruddhaṁ vimārga-vācakotpathādivat jñāna-karmādikaṁ sarvam evoddharmaḥ | tac ca sadā sarvatra prakāśamānasyāpi bhakti-mārgasya guhādau dravyāṇām ivācchādakatvāc chārvaraṁ tamaḥ tad dharati iti tathā | he sva-bhakti-prakāśakety arthaḥ |

tatra tamasi ye caranti ta eva rajanī-cara-sāmyād akhila-lokaika-jīvana-bhakti-mārga-vighātakatvāc ca rākṣasāḥ te ca prāyaśaḥ pṛthivy-antar-gate karma-kṣetre eveti kṣiti-grahaṇam anye ca vṛṣṇi-kulādi-dveṣiṇo ye ye kṣiti-rākṣasāḥ kaṁsādayo ye ca rāsa-krīḍādi-vighātakāḥ śaṅkhacūḍāriṣṭādayas tebhyaḥ sarvebhya eva tat-tad-vighna-vināśāya vicitra-līlayā druhyatīti tathā |

evaṁ kartuṁ ca tvam evārhasi, nānyaḥ ko'pīty āha—arhatīty arhan he sarvaṁ kartuṁ yogya samartheti vā | yat pūrvaṁ nārāyaṇa-rūpeṇa nānāvatāraiś ca tvayā nācaritaṁ tad adhunāviṣkṛtāśeṣa-bhagavattāparā-kāṣṭhākatvāt sampādayituṁ tavaivocitaṁ śakyaṁ cety arthaḥ |

nanu, veda-vihita-svadharma-jñānādi-parāṇāṁ tathā mātulatvādi-sambandhavatāṁ kaṁsādīnāṁ droho'nupayukta eveti cet, tatrāha—he bhagavan parama-kṛpālo ! teṣām eva hitārtham evaṁ karoṣīty arthaḥ |

ayaṁ bhāvaḥ—ye tvad-bhakti-vimukhā jñāna-karma-parāḥ antar-nigūḍhāsura-bhāvāḥ teṣāṁ tad-duḥkha-bhāvāpanayanena ye ca sarva-lokopatāpakāḥ kaṁsādayas teṣāṁ vimukti-pradānena ghāta eva parama-kāruṇikatām abhivyañjayatīti | athavā svāmi-vyākhyaivānusartavyā | ittham eva he arhan ! sarveṣāṁ pūjya ! yato jñāna-karma-parā api bhakti-mārge'dhunā praviṣṭāḥ yathā akrūra-bhīṣmādayaḥ | prakaṭa-daityāś ca kaṁsādayo'ntar-bhayādinā tvad-bhāvanayā bhaktim evāśritāḥ | bahiś ca nija-sahaja-karma-sāmarthya-rahitatvān mṛta-tulyā eva jātā vimuktiṁ vā maraṇena prāptāḥ avaśyam-bhāvitvāt abhūtam api bhūtavat sūcitam | yady evaṁ tvayā na kṛtaṁ syāt, tadā sarvair eva tvaṁ na pūjitaḥ syā iti bhāvaḥ |

evaṁ stuvan nirbhara-prema-rasa-nimagno bhagavatā saha vraja-vāsinaḥ sarvān eva yāsyan namaskaroti | ārkam arka-nāma-vṛkṣa-viśeṣo niṣprayojano bhagavat-pūjānarho vaiṣṇavānām anādaraṇīyaḥ atratyaṁ tam abhivyāpya tubhyaṁ namaḥ, tvāṁ tvadīyāṁś cātratyān sarvān jaṅgamān sthāvarān api namāmīty arthaḥ | etac ca tad bhūri-bhāgyam iha janma kim apy aṭavyām [bhā.pu. 10.14.34] iti nija-prārthanānurūpam eva | yato'tratyo'rka-vṛkṣo'pi man-namasyo matto'dhikatara eva, ato na tādṛśam api janmātrāham arhāmi | atas tasmād apy alpataram anyat kiñcid eva prārthyam iti dhvanitam | tatra ca praṇāmeṣu saṁkhyātṛptir api kathañcin nāstīty ākalpam ity uktam | itthaṁ sarva-śobhā-yukta-kṛṣṇa ! he śrī-kṛṣṇa ! iti ||106||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : he śrī-kṛṣṇeti, śrīḥ sarva-viṣayaka-vividha-śobhā, tad yukta-kṛṣṇa ! śrīmat tvād eva sarvacittākarṣaketi sarvathā sarvato vaiśiṣṭyam abhipretam | tad evābhivyanakti-vṛṣṇīty-ādinā, tatra tatra hetuḥ—bhagavann iti sarvaiśvarya-vistārakety arthaḥ | anyat tair vyākhyātam |

yad vā, itthaṁ sañjāta-parama-bhaktyānte vicitra-nāma-saṅkīrtana-rūpāṁ stutiṁ kurvan sarvam avatāra-prayojanam eka-ślokena saṁkṣipya varṇayan sa-padāvja-grahaṁ praṇamati | tatra vṛṣṇi-kuleti—sūryodayena kamalānāṁ tamomudrā-nirasana-pūrvaka-prakāśanam ivāvirbhāva-mātreṇa śrī-vasudevādi-yādava-vargāṇāṁ duḥkha-nāśana-pūrvakānanda-pradatvaṁ prāg-varṇitam | tac coktam eva sa vibhrat pauruṣaṁ dhāma [bhā.pu 1.2.17] iti diṣṭyā hare’syā bhavataḥ pado bhuvaḥ [bhā.pu 1.2.38] ity-ādinā | kiṁ ca, pṛthivyāś ca devānāṁ ca viprāṇāṁ ca paśūdadheś ca gavādi-paśu-samudrasya śrī-nanda-vrajasya vṛddhi-kārin ! adharma-nivartana-dharma-pravartanādinā sākṣād duḥkha-haraṇa-sukha-sampādanādinā ca vardhanaśīla ! yady api kṣmā-vardhanenaiva tad antar-gatānāṁ dvija-gavādīnāṁ vṛddhi-kāritvaṁ svata evāyāti, tathāpy avatāre’smin tad viśeṣāpekṣayā pṛthaṅ nirdiṣṭam |

yad vā, mahar-lokādivartinām-ṛṣīṇāṁ golākādivarti-gokulānāṁ ca grahaṇārtham | yad vā, vraja-sambandhino ye kṣmā-nirjarā viprā dvijāś ca pakṣiṇaḥ paśavaś ca go-mahiṣyādayo’tiprācuryātta evodadhayas tad-vṛddhi-kārinn iti prādhānyād vrajasyaiva sambardhakatvam uktam |

adhunā prema-bhakti-vistāraṇaṁ mukhya-prayojanam ante varṇayati, dharmo’tra bhakti-lakṣaṇas tad viruddhaṁ jñānādikam evotpathādi-nyāyenodharmaḥ, sa eva sadā sarvatra prakāśamānasyāpi bhakti-mārgasyāc chadakatvāc chārvaraṁ tamas tad dharatīti tathā, ataeva he kṣiti-rākṣasa-dhruk ! kṣiti-rākṣasā dvi-vidhāḥ—eke bhavatam asi ye caranti rajanī-cara-sāmyād akhila-lokaika-jīvana-bhakti-mārga-vighātakatvāc ca rākṣasā anta-nigūḍhāsurabhāva asantaḥ, anye ca vṛṣṇi-kulādi-dveṣiṇaḥ prakaṭa-daityāḥ kaṁsādayas tebhyo druhyatīti tathā, tatrāsatāṁ durmadādi-nigraheṇa kaṁsādīṇaṁ ca sākṣān māraṇenaiva | tac ca teṣāṁ hitārtham evety āha—bhagavan ! he parama-dayālo !—satāṁ bhaktau pravartanāt | etac cāpūrva-dṛṣṭaṁ tvam eva kartum arhasi, nānyaḥ ko’pīty āha—arhatīty arhan he sarvaṁ kartuṁ yogya samartheti vā, ata ākalpaṁ maj jīvana-kālam abhivyāpya | yad vā, ā ākalpaṁ tvadīya-guñjāvataṁsa-barhāpīḍādikaṁ bāma-hastastha-kabalādikaṁ ca bhūṣaṇam abhivyāpya, tathārkam arko nāma vṛkṣo bhagavad anarhapuṣpo vaiṣṇavānām anādaraṇīyas tam arpyakam abhivyāpya tat tat sahitāyāśeṣa-nijaiśvarya-prakaṭana-śobhā-yukta kṛṣṇa paramānanda nanda-nandana ! namaste |

śrīmac caitanya-devānugṛhītānām anugrahāt |

teṣāṁ mude stutir brāhmī vyākhyāteyaṁ yathā-ruci ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : śrīti sādara-sarva-sampadanubhava-pūrvakaṁ praṇāmāya prathamataḥ kṛṣṇeti sva-rūpa-nāmnā sambodhanaṁ punaḥ krameṇa tato bahir bahir viśeṣyaṁ vyañjayan janma-karmanām abhir api sambodhayati, vṛṣṇīty—ādinā | vṛṣṇi-kulaṁ śrī-vasudevādikaṁ śrī-nandādikaṁ ca pūrvokta-prāmānyāt tatra prastutatvena śrī-nandādikam evātra mukhyaṁ jñeyam paśupāṅgajāyeti pratijñātatvāt kṣmety aviśeṣeṇa prāptave’pi viśeṣa-vivakṣayā dvija-paśvor upādānaṁ go-brāhmaṇa-hitāvatāratā-prasiddheḥ uddharmo bhagavad vimukho dharmaḥ kṣiti-rākṣasāḥ uddharma-pravartakāḥ kaṁsādayaś ca candra-sūrya-rūpobhayatvena rūpaka-vyañjanā sarva-śubhakaritā-vivakṣayā kīrti-pratāpa-praśaṁsādyotanecchayā ca he bhagavan ! evam-bhūta-śrī-kṛṣṇa-rūpatvena svayaṁ bhagavann ity arthaḥ | tathārkaṁ svar-bhūva bhūr loka-vyāpaka-prakāśam arkam ārbhya mahā-vaikuṇṭha-paryantam arhan pūjya ! yad vā, evatacāpūrvaṁ dṛṣṭaṁ tvam eva kartum arhasi nānyaḥ ko’pīty āha—arhatīty arhan he sarvaṁ kartuṁ yogya ! samartheti vā, ata ākalpaṁ maj jīvana-kāla-rūpān kalpān abhivyāpya yad vā, ākalpaṁ tvadīya-guñjāvataṁsa-barhāpīḍādikaṁ vāma-hasta-sthaka-valādikaṁ ca bhūṣaṇam abhivyāpya tubhyaṁ tathā ārkam armo nāma-vṛkṣo bhagavad anarha-puṣpo vaiṣṇavānām anādaranīyaḥ tam apy antaṁ samabhivyāpya tat tat sahitāyety arthaḥ |

śrīmac-caitanya-devānugṛhītānām anugrahāt |

teṣāṁ mude stutir brāhmī vyākhyāteyaṁ yathā-mati ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : tasmād bhūyo’pi te nama iti sa-stavaṁ praṇamati—śrī-kṛṣṇeti | he bhagavan ! namas te | vṛṣṇi-kula-puṣkarāṇāṁ joṣaḥ prītis tāṁ dātuṁ śīlaṁ yasyety anena bhānutvaṁ vyaṅgyam | kṣmā pṛthvī | nirjarā devāḥ | dvijāḥ paśavaś ca vṛndāvana-sambandhinas ta evodadhayaḥ | duravagāhyatvāt | teṣāṁ vṛddhi-kāritvena candratvam | ūrdhvamā eva śārvarāṇi tamāṁsi teṣāṁ haratvena mahā-jyotiṣṭvād vahnitvaṁ vahnīndv-arkās tamonudaḥ ity ukteḥ | ata eka eva tvaṁ bhānu-candra-vahnīnāṁ maṇḍalāni | ayam arthaḥ—yathā amī tejo-maṇḍala-rūpeṇa bahiḥ prakāśante, antas tu sva-svākāreṇa vartante, tathā tvam api brahmākāreṇa viśva-vyāpī dṛśyamānākāreṇa paricchinna iti | kṣiti-rākṣasa-dhruk | tvam etat-tritaya-tejo-vyatirikta-nija-tejo-viśeṣavattvam | ārkam arkam abhivyāpya | namo yāvat sūryo bhāsyati | tāvat-kāla-paryantaṁ nama ity arthaḥ | kiṁ bahunā ? ākalpaṁ kalpo dviparārdha-kālaṁ yāvaj jīvāmi tāvad eva nama iti bhāvaḥ | he arhan namasya ! evaṁ naumīty upakramya namasta ity upasaṁhāraḥ ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : yadyapi mām aparādhinaṁ vijñāya na brūṣe tad api sva-netrābhyāṁ sānugrahāvalokanāmṛtaṁ tu mahyaṁ dehi yathā tenaivāhāreṇa nityaṁ prāṇān rakṣan kalpa-paryantaṁ jīvituṁ prabhaviṣyāmīti vyañjayan praṇamati—śrī-kṛṣṇeti | sūryasva-rūpaṁ dakṣiṇaṁ netram ālakṣyāha—vṛṣṇi-kula-padmasya joṣaḥ praphullatvaṁ tat-pradāyin mām api padma-santānaṁ kṛpayā praphullayeti bhāvaḥ | candra-svarūpaṁ vāma-netram ālakṣyāha—kṣmā kṣmā-talasthā manuṣyādayaḥ nirjarāḥ svarga-sthāḥ devā dvijāḥ paśavaś ca vṛndāvana-sthāḥ pakṣiṇo gāvaś ca ta evodadhayas teṣāṁ vṛddhi-kārin ! mām api devādhamaṁ kṛpayā varddhayeti bhāvaḥ | yugapad eva netra dve eva puṣpavantāvālakṣyāha—uddharmaḥ pāṣaṇḍa-dharmaḥ sa eva śārvaram andhatamasaṁ śāvaraṁ tv andhatamasam ity amaraḥ | tad dharatīti tathā tena sva-prabhau tvayy api māyā-cikīrṣā-lakṣaṇaṁ mama pāṣaṇḍaṁ kṛpayā hara yathā punar evaṁ na kuryāmiti bhāvaḥ | kṣitau rākṣasā aghāsurādayas tebhyo druhasi droheṇāpi sva-gatiṁ dadāsīty atas tvad dvayasya vṛndavatsa-vṛndavidrohitvāt satya-loka-brahma-rākṣasaṁ mām api daṇḍa-pradānenāpi saṁskuruṣv eti bhāvaḥ | sva-prabhor anugrahaṁ vā dṛṣṭvā dāso jīvitum utsahate audāsīnyaṁ dṛṣṭvā na prāṇān dhartuṁ īṣṭe iti bhāvaḥ | hanta hanta mahāmaheśvaro'pi kṣetra-guñjā-gaurika-picchādir acitākalpo go-cāraka-bālakaiḥ samaṁ khelan hṛṣyatīty anaucityaṁ mat-prabhor iti pūrva vicāritaktā tattvān-abhijñena mayā yeṣv aparāddhaṁ tān api prasādayāmīti manasi vibhāvyāh—ā ākalpaṁ tvadīya-gujādi-veṣam abhivyāpya ārkaṁ arko nāma vṛkṣo bhagavad anarha puṣpas tam api vajastham abhivyāpya he arhana, mat-pūjya ! kiṁ vā he yogya kṛpākṛpābhyāṁ mad bhadrābhadraṁ kartuṁ samartha ! te tattā hitāya tubhyaṁ namaḥ--

sarva-saṁśaya-hṛt sarva-bhakti-siddhānta-santatiḥ |

astu brahma-stutiś citta-bhittau me cāru citritā ||40||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : yadyapi sāgasaṁ māṁ na-santāpayethās tathāpi kṛpāvalokaṁ vinā me jīvitaṁ durddharam iti vyañjayan praṇamati śrī-kṛṣṇeti | vṛṣṇi-kulam eva puṣkaraṁ tasya joṣo vikāsas tad dāyiniti sūryatvaṁ tena mam āpi padma-bhavatvāt tad dānaṁ sāmprataṁ kṣamā ca nirjarāś ca dvijāś ca paśavo gāvaś ca ta evodadhis tad-vṛddhi-kārinn-iti-candratvaṁ tena mamāpi nirjara-dvijasya vṛddhi-karaṇaṁ sāmprataṁ kṣitau rākṣasebhyo aghādibhyā druhyasi droheṇāpi sva-padaṁ dadāsīti atas tvat sa-jagdhi-pratyūha-kāriṇaṁ brahma-rākṣasaṁ mām api daṇḍena saṁskṛtya sva-padaṁ dehi tyajaudāsīnya tena maj-jīvanaṁ duddharam iti bhāvaḥ | kiñ ca sarveśvarasyānanta-lakṣmīkasya guñjā-piñchādi-veṣo go-cāraṇaṁ gopabhis teḥ saha sakhyañ ca nocitam ity aviśena mayā manasi sambhāvyāparāddhaṁ tad idānīṁ kṣamayāmīty āha—ākalpaṁ gujādi-veṣam abhivyāpya arkas tvad anarha-puṣpo vṛkṣā brajasthas taṁ cābhivyāpya tat-sahitāya te namo'ste tat-tat-sarvasya tvadīyatvādvi jñāna-ghanatvāc ca mad vandyatvam | he arhana pūjya ||40||

paramaiśvarya-mādhurya bhakti-bhṛd brahmaṇoditā |

jīyād gokula-māhātmya dhātrī-nanda-sutas tatiḥ ||

…

|| 10.14.41 ||

śrī-śuka uvāca—

ity abhiṣṭhūya bhūmānaṇ triḥ parikramya pādayoḥ |

nanv abhīṣṭaṁ jagad-dhātā sva-dhāma pratyapadyata ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) :

sa-sakhi-vatsaka-mokṣaṇa-kautuka-druta-vilaṁbita-putram ude'dbhutam |

nikhila-rūpam ato dadhad acyuto'vatu sa vaḥ kalayan vraja-maṁgalam ||1||

pādayor natvā | abhīṣṭaṁ sarvataḥ pūjitam abhipretaṁ vā ||41||

————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : sa-sakha-vatsaka-mokṣaṇena yat-kautukaṁ tena drutaṁ gataṁ vilambitaṁ vilaṁbo yasya sa tathā | yad vā, drutaḥ śīghratā-yutaś cāsau vilambito jāta-vilambaś cati druta-vilamvitaḥ vatsa-pālāvalokanārthaṁ bhagavad avalokanārthaṁ vā prathamaṁ druto dṛṣṭvā ca saṁśayitaḥ san vilambito'bhūd iti, sa cāsau putro brahmā ceti tathā | yad vā, dravati nisargeṇa nimnaṁ gacchatīti drutaṁ toyam, drute vilambate ūrdha-deśe tiṣṭhatīti druta-vilambitaṁ kamalam, tasya putras tathā tasya mude | yad vā, druto'tivegavān viḥ pakṣī haṁso yasya sa druta-vibrahmā, laṁbanaṁ laṁbā krīḍā, sā ca-jaṁghādho. bhāgena laghu kāṣṭha-daṇḍaṁ dūre nikṣipya tad ānayanārtha-gata-bālāt-prāg eva vṛkṣa-śākhāṁ gṛhītvā tato'py ūrdhvaṁ-śākhām ālaṁbana-rūpā, saiva markaṭotplavana-śabdena lakṣitā, sā ca gopāleṣu prāyo'sti, laṁbā jātā yeṣāṁ te laṁbitās te putrā yasya gopī-janasya sa laṁbita-putraḥ, sa-sakha-vatsaka-mokṣaṇena kautukaṁ yasya sa tathā sa cāsau drutaviś ceti tathā, sa-sakha-vatsaka-mokṣaṇa-kautuka-drutaviś ca laṁbita-putraś ceiti samāhāra-dandvas tasya mude tan-mātṛṇāṁ ca kasya ca ity ukteḥ | yato nikhila-rūpaṁ rūpe nirūpe nikhilaṁ nikhila-rūpaṁ nikhilānāṁ rūpam iti vā dadhat ato hetoḥ vraja-maṁgala kalayan racayan saḥ prasiddho'neka-rūpatvepy acyuto nirvikāraḥ vo'dhyetradhyāpakān avatu |

bhūmānaṁ sarveśvaram | tir-abhiṣṭya veti saṁbandhaḥ | yathoktānvaye tu ekā caṇḍyā raveḥ sapta tisro gaṇapateḥ smṛtāḥ catasraḥ keśavasyoktāḥ śivasyārdhā pradakṣiṇā | iti virodhaḥ syāt | sarvataḥ pūjyatve nāstikābhāva-prasaṅga manvāno'rthāntaraṁ brūte—abhipretaṁ veti | sva-dhāma satya-lokam | praty apadyata prāpa | bhūmānaṁ sarvathaivāparicchinnam | abhīṣṭaṁ bhagavatā prasthāpayim iti śeṣaḥ| yato jagad-dhātā anyathā sahasā tat-pada-tyāge viśva-sṛṣṭer asiddheḥ, tataś ca yāvad adhikāram avasthitir ādhikārikāṇām iti nyāyenādhikārānte tad abhīṣṭaṁ setsyatīti buddhayate ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : bhūmānam aparicchinna-māhātmyam | ity evam abhiṣṭūya abhiṣṭutyābhitaḥ stutvā | yad vā, sarva-vyāpakam api tathāvasthitaṁ bhaktyā triḥ parikramyābhīṣṭaṁ priyatamaṁ sva-dhāma satya-lokavarti-nija-gṛhaṁ gata iti prathamaṁ lajjādinā vraja-vāsa-lobhena vā, pārameṣṭhyaṁ padaṁ parityajya kutrāpi nilīya sthāsyāmīti manasi kṛtvā sthitaḥ, idānīṁ ca śrī-bhagavat-prasādam āsādya nija-priya-padam evāgacchad ity arthaḥ, yato jagad-dhātā, anyathā tat pradacyutā viśva-sṛṣṭya-siddhe ataḥ śrī-bhagavān eva prasthāpayāmāsa ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : bhūmānaṁ sarvathaivāparicchinnam ity evam abhiṣṭuya abhiṣṭutya abhitaḥ stutvā | yad vā, sarva-vyāpakam eva tathāvasthitaṁ bhaktyā tri-parikramya abhīṣṭaṁ sāmīpyayāñcāyāṁ kṛta-maunena śrī-kṛṣṇenābhipretaṁ dhāma yato jagad-dhātā anyathā tat pada-tyāge viśva-sṛṣṭy asiddheḥ evaṁ yāvad adhikāram avasthitir ādhikāriṇām [3.3.31] iti nyāyena tad ante tad abhīṣṭa-siddhir bhaviṣyatīti jñāpyate ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbha) : abhīṣṭa-sāmīpyayāñcāyāṁ kṛtam aunena śrī-bhagavatābhipretaṁ sva-dhāma bhūmānam evābhīṣṭam ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : abhīṣṭaṁ bhagavatā prasthāpayitum iti śeṣaḥ, yato jagad-dhātā anyathā sahasā tat padatyājane viśva-sṛṣṭya-siddheḥ tataś ca [3.3.31] yāvad adhikāram avasthitir ādhikārikāṇām iti nyāyenādhikārānte tad abhīṣṭaṁ setsyatīti buddhyate ||41||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : evaṁ madhura-stuti-nirgarva-vraja-bhāvābhivāñchāparituṣṭena bhagavatā kṛpayā avalokito virañcir bhūmānaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ punaḥ kiñcid abhiṣṭūya niḥ parikramya pādau natvā samprati jagatkāryaṁ kuru tad avasāne yāvad adhikāra nyāyena vābhīṣṭaṁ bhābīti tenoktaḥ sva-dhāma pratyapadyata prāpa ||41||

...

|| 10.14.42 ||

tato’nujñāpya bhagavān sva-bhuvaṁ prāg-avasthitam |

vatsān pulinam āninye yathā-pūrva-sakhaṁ svakam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : anujñāpyānujñāṁ pradāpya, pṛṣṭvety arthaḥ | sva-bhūvaṁ brahmāṇam | yathā-pūrvaṁ sakhāyo yasmiṁs tat svakaṁ pulinam prāg-avasthitān-vanaṁ prati pūrvavad avasthitān-vatsānāninye ||42||

————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tataḥ stavanānantaram | ity artha iti | prayojaka-nijantāj jñaper-lyap nānye mito’hetau iti mitvābhāva ihāyam artho bodhyaḥ pradāpya dāpayitveti | vastutaḥ sva-bhuvaṁ svodbhūtam api manjuṣya-nāṭyābhiprāyeṇa loka-saṅgrahārthaṁ vā pṛṣṭavān iti bhāvaḥ | sva-bhūvaṁ maunenaivānujñāpya anujānīhi māṁ kṛṣṇa [bhā.pu. 12.6.6] ity ājñāprārthane kṛte maunaṁ sammati-lakṣaṇam iti bramaṇā sahasāvagamāt | mauna-tyāgābhāvastu—paśupavaśa-śiśutvad aśāyām aṅgoikṛtasya brahma-mohanārtham aṅgīkṛtasya nāṭyasyārambhaparisamāpti-siddhyarthaḥ | tatra

tato vatsān adṛṣṭvaitya puline’pi ca vatsapān |

ubhāv api vane kṛṣno vicikāya samantataḥ ||[bhā.pu. 10.13.16]

iti vatsa-bālākānveṣaṇa-nāṭyārambhaḥ | naumīḍya [bhā.pu. 10.14.1] ity-ādi-brahma-stutau pravṛttāyāṁ kutastyoyaṁ caturmukhaḥ kiṁ tiṣṭhate kiṁ vā, muhur brūte iti svavatsān veṣaṇa-vyagro’haṁ gopa-śiśur na budhye iti vyañjakena maunenaiva tasyaiva nāṭyasya samāptir iti svādhīna-brahmaṇogre nija-mahaiśvaryasyājñānam abhinīyate smeti tan nāṭya-śabdenocyate | tatrodvahat paśupa-vaṁśa-śiśutva-nāṭyam ity-ādinā vātsalya-rasa-parikara-vrajeśvaryādīnām agre tu tat-premādhīnena śrī-kṛṣṇena nija-mahaiśvaryasya tan mahā-prema-mādhurya-rasācchāditasyājñāna-yāthārthyam eva, tatra na tasyābhinaya iti na tan nāṭya-śabdena vācyam iti vivecanīyam | prāk prāg eva | tṛṇa-caraṇādi-ceṣṭādibhir avasthitān svakaṁ sva-bhojana-sthānaṁ pulinam āninye | yathā-pūrva pūrvopaveśādikam anatikramyāparityajya vartamānāḥ sakhāyo yatra tat, samāsānta ārṣaḥ |

yad vā, yathā yathāvad eva sthitāḥ pūrva-sakhāḥ svarūpa-bhūta-sakhebhyaḥ pūrva-sakhāyo yatra tat | etat sarva-samādhānaṁ ca śrī-kṛṣṇecchā-samvalitamāyā-vaibhavam eva, tathā mātrādibhir vyavahāraupayikaṁ hyastanādinā tat-tad bālakādi-caritaṁ smṛtam api prācīneṣu smāraytuṁ neṣṭam ity-ādi bodhyam ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : na ca kevalaṁ svecchayā gata ity āha—tata iti | anujñāpya prasthāpyety arthaḥ | sva-bhuvam ātmajam iti sarvāparādha-kṣamādikaṁ sūcitaṁ | tato brahmānujñāpanānantaram eva, prāk pūrvam eva, yathā-pūrva-sakhaṁ svakaṁ nija-bhojana-sthānaṁ vatsānāṁ pūrvāvasthiti-sthānaṁ vā, saraḥ-pulinam avasthitān vanāntar-vartamānān vatsān ānināya | itthaṁ ca śrī-brahmaṇānīya samarpitā iti śrī-bhagavataiva vā kuto’pi savatsā bālakā ānītās te vā, svayaṁ kuto’py āgatya punas tatropaviṣṭā ity uktyabhāvāt kevalaṁ māyāpagamena pūrvavat puline bhojanārtham āsīneṣu pūrvam eva tānāśvasya vatsānayanārthaṁ gato’sau vatsānevādhunānītavān ity ukteś ca māyā-mohitāḥ santo’bdaṁ yāvat te tatra tatrāvartanteti spaṣṭam evāvagamyate, na ca vyaktam idam anyair janair gopādibhiḥ kathaṁ te tatra na dṛṣṭāḥ ? śītavātādinā vā kathaṁ na hi vādhitā iti, māyāyāḥ sarva-śaktimatvāt? yadi vā vaktavyam idam—śrī-bhagavatā dṛṣṭvāpi tādṛg avasthās te katham upekṣitāḥ syur ataḥ śrī-brahmaṇā satya-lokādau śrī-bhagavad adṛśya-sthāne kutrāpi nītvā māyayā mohayitvā rakṣitā iti ? tan na yuktam, yato brahma-mude na tesamānītā ity ādāv uktam eva, māhāśaye [bhā.pu 10.13.41] ity uktyā śrī-bhagavat-kṛpayā ca teṣāṁ duḥkhābhāva eva, yatra kutrāpi nītā api jñāna-ghana-mūrteḥ śrī-bhagavato naivādṛśyāḥ syur nāma, pūrvantu yat kṣaṇaṁ na te dṛṣṭā itas tato mārgitāś ca tat-snehaparavaśatayā kiṁ vā, śrī-brahma-mohārtham evety api pūrvam uktam eveti sarvathā te tatra tatraiva tathā sthitā iti susidhye | ata eva śrī-svāmi-pādair api likhitam—nanv etāvantaṁ kālaṁ kathaṁ te tatraivopaviṣṭhāḥ ? kathaṁ ca kṣutpipāsādi-vismaraṇam ity-ādi | yathā-pūrvaṁ sakhāyo yasminn iti prāg avasthitān iti ca bālaka-vatsa-pulina-bhojya-bhojanārthopaveśādikam api sarvaṁ pūrvavad eva vartamānaṁ bodhayati, kintv ābdikavasasā kiyatyākārādi-vṛddhir jñeyā | tathā mātrādiṣu tadvadbālakācaritādikaṁ śrī-bhagavat-prabhāveṇa teṣu nijatayā parisphuritam iti ca boddhavyam anyathābdasthiter āgantukais taiḥ saha vailakṣaṇyāpatter iti dik ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : sva-bhuvam ātmajam iti sarvāparādha-kṣamādikaṁ sūcitam anujñāpya idānīm api ānayānīti sasmitaṁ pṛṣṭvā anujñāpanaṁ cedaṁ sva-sārvajñādi-vyāñjanopālambhanotprāpta-śikṣā-kṣamānugraha-vinayādi-vyāñjakam apīdaṁ vyañjayati, tādṛśa-svarūpair api bāla-vatsair na mama līlā-sukhaṁ kintu tair eveti tato brahmānujñāpanānantaraṁ prāk prāg vad evāvasthāceṣṭādibhir avasthitān yathā-pūrvaṁ pūrvavad avasthā-ceṣṭādy anatikrameṇa vartamānāḥ sakhāyo yatra tat svakaṁ sva-bhojana-sthānaṁ pulinam-āninye atra sa-kavala-pāṇeḥ kṛṣṇasyāgatasya taiḥ pūrvavad eva dṛṣśyamānasyāvasthitiś ca pūrvavad eva jñeyā etat sarva-samādhānaṁ ca śrī-kṛṣṇecchāsambalita-māyā-vaibhavam eva tathā mātrādibhir vyavahāraupayikaṁ hyastanādi tat tad bālakācaritaṁ smṛtam api prācīneṣu smārayituṁ neṣṭam ity api boddham ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : anujñāpanena caivaṁ jñāpitaṁ tādṛśa-svarūpair api bāla-vatsair na mama līlā-sukhaṁ kintu tair eveti svakaṁ nija-bhojana-sthānaṁ yathā-pūrva-sakham iti prāk prāg-vad evāvasthitān iti ||42.44||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : sva-bhūvaṁ brahmāṇam anujñāpya ajñāpyeti maunenaiva anujānīhi māṁ kṛṣṇety ājñā-prārthane kṛte maunaṁ sammati-lakṣaṇam iti brahmaṇā sahasāvagamāt maunatyāgābhāvas tu paśupa-vaṁśa-śiśutvadvaśāyām aṅgī-kṛtasya brahma-mohanārthe nāṭyasyārambha-parisamāpti-siddhyarthe tatra

tato vatsān adṛṣṭvaitya puline’pi ca vatsapān |

ubhāvapi vane kṛṣṇo vicikāya samantataḥ ||[bhā.pu. 10.13.16]

iti vatsa-bālakānveṣaṇa-nāṭyārambhe naumīḍyety-ādi-brahma-stutau pravṛttāyāṁ kutastyo’yaṁ caturmukhaḥ kiñceṣṭate vā muhur-brūte iti sva-vatsānveṣaṇa-vyagro’haṁ gopa-śiśur na budhyate iti-vyañjakena maunenaiva tasyaiva nāṭyasya parisamāptir iti svādhīna-brahmaṇo’gre kṛṣṇena nija-mahaiśvaryasyājñānam abhinīyate smeti tan nāṭya-śabdenocyate tatrodvahat paśupa-vaṁśa-śiśutva-nāṭyam ity-ādinā vātsalyādi-rasa-parikara-vrajeśvaryādīnām agre tu tan mahā-premādhīne kṛṣṇe nija-mahaiśvaryasya tan mahā-prema-mādhurya-rasācchāditasyājñānaṁ yathāryam eveti tatra na tasyābhinaya iti na tan nāṭya-śabde vācam iti vivecanīyaṁ prāk prāgvad eva tṛṇa-caraṇādi-ceṣṭābhir avasthitān svakaṁ sva-bhojana-sthānaṁ pulinam āninye kīdṛśaṁ yathā-pūrvaṁ pūrvopaveśādikam anatikramya aparityajya vartamānāḥ sakhāyo yatra tat samāsānta ārṣaḥ | yad vā, yathāvad eva sthitāḥ pūrva-sakhāḥ svarūpa-bhūta-sakhebhyaḥ pūrve sakhāyo yatra tat ||42||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : gṛhīta-mauna eva bhagavān dṛg-antenaiva sva-bhuvaṁ brahmāṇam anujñāpya sva-lokaṁ gacchety ājñāpya vatsān svakaṁ sva-sa-jagdhi-sthānaṁ pulinam ānaninye, kīdṛśān prāk pūrvavad eva tṛṇa-bhakṣaṇādi-ceṣṭayāvasthitān pulinaṁ kīdṛśaṁ yathā yathāvad eva sthitāḥ pūrva-sakhāḥ prakāśita-sakhyebhyaḥ pūrve prakṛtāḥ sakhyāyo yatra tat ||42||

...

|| 10.14.43 ||

ekasminn api yāte’bde prāṇeśaṁ cāntarātmanaḥ |

kṛṣṇa-māyā-hatā rājan kṣaṇārdhaṁ menire’rbhakāḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : nanv etāvantaṁ kālaṁ kathaṁ tatraivopaviṣṭāḥ kathaṁ ca kṣut-pipāsādi-vismaraṇam ? ata āha—ekasminn iti śloka-dvayena | abde’pi yāte prāṇeśaṁ kṛṣṇam antarā ca vināpi | priya-virahe kṣaṇo’pi varṣādhiko bhavati, tathāpi māyā-hatāḥ kṣaṇārdhaṁ menire ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : kautukāviṣṭaḥ śaṅkate—nanv iti | te gopāḥ | tatraiva puline | abde varṣe | ātmano jīva-mātrasya | prāṇeśaṁ svāminam | māyātra yoga-māyā tayaiva sarveṣāṁ gopa-gopī-nandādīnām abda-kālo’pi muhūrtayāmadinamāsartvādi-parivartiṁ vinaiva pañca-pālātmaka-kṣaṇārdha-kāla eva sādhitam iti na tasyā acintya-vaibhavāyāḥ kim api duḥsampādyam iti ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ātmanaḥ svasya prāṇeśam ātmanaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasya māyā-hatā iti vā, māyā yadyapi brāhmy eva, tathāpi śrī-bhagavatānumoditā satī bhāgavaty eva saṁvṛttā | ataḥ prāk śrī-baladeva-cintane prāyo māyās tu me bhartuḥ [bhā.pu 10.13.37] iti, evaṁ sarvam eva saṅgacchate | tac ca pūrvaṁ likhitam eva, atas tayā-hatā vyāptāḥ | prāṇeśatve hetuḥ—kṛṣṇaṁ śrī-vrajendrakumāra, | kiṁ ca, arbhakāḥ śrī-nanda-vraja-bālakāḥ | he rājann iti paramādbhutatvāt ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tathā teṣāṁ kālājñānaṁ ca māyayaivety āha—ātmanaḥ | svasya prāṇeśam ātmanaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasya māyā-hatā iti vā, māyā yady api brāhmy eva tathāpi śrī-bhagavatānumoditā satī bhāgavaty eva saṁvṛtteti ataḥ śrī-baladeva-cintane prāyo māyās tu me bhartuḥ [bhā.pu 10.13.37] iti tat tac tac cāsambhavam api tad icchā-śakteḥ sarva-śaktito balabattvān nātyasambhavam atas tayā hatāḥ pratibaddhāḥ mano-hataḥ pratihataḥ pratibaddho hataś ca saḥ ity amaraḥ | prāṇeśatve hetuḥ kṛṣṇaṁ ballavendra-kumāram | kiṁ ca, arbhakāḥ śrī-nanda-vraja-bālakāḥ kṣaṇārdhaṁ palapañcakaṁ menire tatra teṣāṁ pratītau muhūrtayām adivasaṛtvādi-parivartaṁ tasya kālasya sthite he rājann iti paramādbhutatvāt ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : atra tāvat kālājñānaṁ tathaiva kavala-pāṇeḥ kṛṣṇasyāgatasya taiḥ saha tathaiva bhojana-līlā-śeṣādikaṁ dustarka-yoga-māyā-vaibhavam evety āha—ekasminn ity-ādi-caturbhiḥ | ātmanaḥ svasya prāṇeśaṁ kṛṣṇam antarā vināpi yoga-māyayā āhatā āvṛtāḥ ||43||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : atra samayāparijñānaṁ tathaivāgatasya kavala-pāṇer nanda-sūnās taiḥ saha sa-jagdhi-līlā-samāpanaṁ ca tasyācintya-yoga-māyā-vilasitam evāha—ekasminn iti caturbhiḥ | ātmanaḥ svasya prāṇeśaṁ kṛṣṇam antarā vinā kṛṣṇasya māyayā yogākhyayā āhatā āvṛtā kṣaṇārdha-palapañcakam ||43||

...

|| 10.14.44 ||

kiṁ kiṁ na vismarantīha māyā-mohita-cetasaḥ |

yan-mohitaṁ jagat sarvam ābhīkṣṇaṁ vismṛtātmakam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : yad yayā māyayā mohitaṁ jagad abhīkṣṇaṁ punaḥ punar vismṛtātmakaṁ vismṛta ātmaiva yena tat tathā ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : iha saṁsāre abhīkṣṇaṁ suṣuptau śāstre vā jñatasyāpi deha-dvayātiriktasya tasya muhur vismaraṇād iti yoga-māyaiva bahiraṅga-māyātvenātra jñeyā ato bhagavad icchā-balāyās tasyās tādṛśeṣv api mohanatvaṁ ghaṭata eveti bhāvaḥ ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : iha jagati vismṛta ātmaiva | kiṁ vā, sarva-vyāpako bhagavān api yena tat | sarvam itīndrādi-mohāpekṣayā | abhīkṣnam iti kathañcit kadācit smṛtasya smāritasyāpi vā, muhur vismaraṇād iti moha-mahimoktaḥ ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tatra dṛṣṭāntam āha—kiṁ kiṁ iti | iha jagati abhīkṣṇam iti suṣuptau śāstre vā, jñātasyāpi deha-dvayātiriktasya tasya muhur vismaraṇāt iti māyā-mahimoktaḥ atā bhagavad icchā-balāt tasyās tādṛśeṣv api mohanatvaṁ ghaṭeta iti bhāvaḥ ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : mohana-sādharmyeṇa yoga-māyayā bahiraṅga-māyāṁ dṛṣṭāntayati—kiṁ kim iti | vismṛta ātmā yena tat tathaiva yoga-māyayā varṣaṁ vāpya kṛṣṇa-viraha-duḥkhaṁ te vismāritā iti bhāvaḥ ||44||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : mohana-sādharmyeṇa yogamāyāṁ triguṇāṁ dṛṣṭāntayati—kiṁ kim iti | iha jagati māyayā triguṇayā mohitāni cetāṁsi yeṣāṁ te kiṁ kiṁ na vismaranti abhīkṣṇam iti vismṛtaḥ suṣuptau śāstre vā, deha-dvayātiriktatayā jñāto’py ātmā yena tādṛśaṁ bhavati tathā ca varṣaṁ vyāpya kṛṣṇa-viccheda-duḥkhaṁ sakhāyo yoga-māyayā visasmarur iti ||44||

...

|| 10.14.45 ||

ūcuś ca suhṛdaḥ kṛṣṇaṁ svāgataṁ te’tiraṁhasā |

naiko’py abhoji kavala ehītaḥ sādhu bhujyatām ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : ata evaṁ ūcuś ca, te tvayātivegena svāgataṁ samyag āgatam | eko’pi grāsas tvāṁ vināsmābhir nābhoji na bhuktaḥ | ita ehi, sādhu avikṣepeṇa bhūjyatām iti ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : ato māyā-mohitatvāt | svāgataṁ sarva-vatsānayana-pūrvakaṁ sukhenāgatam ity arthaḥ | evaṁ varṣe’py atīte tathaiva kavalādi-sthitiḥ śrī-kṛṣnecchayaiva jāteti jñeyam | atiraṁhasā iti | dūra-gata-vatsānayane ghaṭikaikā tv avaśyaṁ gamiṣyatīty asmābhir vicāritam, tvayā tu kṣaṇārdhenaiva āgatam iti bhāvaḥ | eko’pi grāsas tvayā nābhoji śrī-haste pūrva-kavala-vṛtteḥ | ata itaḥ asmin sarveṣām asmākaṁ maṇḍala-sthāne ehi praviśa sādhu vatsādy-anveṣaṇe vaiyagryaṁ tvaktvā samyag yathā syāt, tathā bhujyatām iti toṣaṇī-cakravartī-kārau ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : svāgataṁ sarva-vatsa-vargānayana-pūrvakaṁ sukhenāgatam ity arthaḥ | anyat tair vyākhyātam | yad vā, atiraṁhasā svāgatam ity asya lakṣaṇam | naiko’pi iti | yad vā, tvayā eko’pi iti | yad va, tvayaiko’pi kavalo nābhoji, śrī-haste pūrva-kavala-vṛtteḥ | ata ito’smin sarveṣām asmākaṁ maṇḍala-madhya-sthāne ehi praviśa | sādhu nijodara-paripūraṇādi-prakāreṇa samyag yathā syāt, tathā bhujyatām ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : svāgataṁ sarva-vatsa-vargānayana-pūrvakaṁ sukhenāgatam ity arthaḥ | evaṁ varṣe’py atīte tathaiva kavalādi-sthitiḥ śrī-bhagavad icchā-vaibhavena jñeyā | anyat taiḥ |

yad vā, tvayāpy eko’pi kavalo nābhoji śrī-haste pūrva-kavala-vṛtteḥ | ata itaḥ asmin sarveṣām asmākaṁ maṇḍala-madhya-sthāne ehi praviśa sādhu vatsādy-anveṣaṇe sampraty api tat-sambhālane vā jāta-vaiyāgryaṁ tyaktvā samyak yathā syāt, tathā bhujyatām ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : ūcuś ca suhṛdaḥ kṛṣṇaṁ svāgataṁ te’tiraṁhasā iti ca vatsān ito vatsapān nītvā anyatrety anenānya atra sakṛd īkṣitānām api teṣāṁ punas tatrāvasthāpanaṁ, teṣāṁ tu tad api na jñātam āsīd iti jñāpayati, atra sarvā hy anupapattir māyayaiva vāritā | sā ca māyā brahmaṇo’pi śrī-bhagavad-anumoditatvāt tadīyaiva jāteti jñeyam | ataḥ śrī-saṅkarṣaṇenoktam prāyo māyās tu me bhartuḥ [bhā.pu. 10.13.37] ity abhīkṣṇaṁ yayā mohitam ||45.46||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : arbhakā ūcuḥ--atiraṁhasā sukhenaivāgato dura-gata-vatsānayane ghaṭikaikā tv avaśyaṁ bhaviṣyatīty asmābhir vicāritaṁ | tvayā tu kṣaṇārdhenaivāgatam iti bhāvaḥ | eko’pi kavalo grāsas tvayā vinā nābhoji tasmād ita ehi ||45||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : ūcuś ceti | tvayā atiraṁhasā kṣaṇārdhenaiva sukhenāgataṁ asmābhis tu ghaṭikāvaśyaṁbhāvinīti dṛṣṭaṁ | tvayā vinā asmābhir eko’pi kavalo nābhoji tasmād ita ehi ||45||

...

|| 10.14.46 ||

tato hasan hṛṣīkeśo’bhyavahṛtya sahārbhakaiḥ |

darśayaṁś carmājagaraṁ nyavartata vanād vrajam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : abhyavahṛtya bhuktvā |

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tato bālokti-śravaṇānantaram | ājagaram aghāsura-deha-sambandhi | hasann iti tad ānanda-darśanāt | abhyavahṛtyeti | varṣe gate’py atra vyañjanādīnāṁ kṣaṇārdha-mātra-parṇāmitvaṁ jātam | darśayann ity āha—he sakhāyaḥ adya mṛto’yaṁ sarpaḥ vasāraktādikalilo vartata iti paśyata tad vadhasya vraje prasyāpanārtham etāvat kāla-paryantaṁ tat tad ācchādanaṁ sarvaṁ yoga-māyā-kṛtyam eveti ||46||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : hasann iti teṣāṁ māyā-mugdha-vākya-śravaṇāt kiṁ vā, duḥkhādivyañjakoktyaśravaṇena praharṣodayād arbhakaiḥ sahābhyavahṛtyeti tair vinā svayaṁ tenaitāvantaṁ kālaṁ kim api nābhojīti sūcayati |

nanu, tarhi śrī-yaśodādibhis tat kathaṁ soḍham ? tatrāha—hṛṣīkeśaḥ sarvendriya-vṛtti-pravartaka iti, bhujyatām ity anukārādi-cāturyeṇa teṣāṁ citte grāhitam ity arthaḥ, yathā śrī-lakṣmaṇena śrī-raghunāthādiṣu, tad-vṛttaṁ ca purāṇeṣu prasiddham eva | yad vā, tadānīṁ svayaṁ śrīti-rahito’pi kevalaṁ teṣāṁ santoṣārtham abhuṅkta, idānīṁ ca nija-rasanendriyādi-pravartanena tat tad-rasa-gandhādi-grahaṇa-pūrvakaṁ paramaprītya bhuṅktaivety arthaḥ | vrajaṁ prati nyavartata, nivavarteti pāṭhe sa evārthaḥ | ājagaraṁ carma darśayann ity aghāsura-vadhasya vraje kathanāya tat-smaraṇārtham iti tac carmāpi śrī-bhagavatā tāvat-kālaṁ māyācchādya rakṣitam āsīd iti jñeyam ||46||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : hasann iti teṣāṁ māyā-mugdhānāṁ vākya-śravaṇāt duḥkhādi-vyañjakokti-śravaṇena praharṣodayāc ca hṛṣīkeśa iti teṣāṁ parama-preṣṭhatvāt vrajaṁ prati vyavartata, niravarted iti pāṭhe sa evārthaḥ | parasmaipadam ārṣam ājagaraṁ carma darśayann ity aghāsura-vadhasya vraje kathanāya iti tac carmāpi śrī-bhagavatā tāvat kālaṁ māyayācchādya tathaiva rakṣitam āsīd iti jñeyam ||46||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : hasann iti, teṣām ānanda-darśanāt abhyavahṛtyeti varṣe gate’py anna-vyañjanādīnāṁ kṣaṇārdha-mātra-pariṇāmitvaṁ jātaṁ tac ca na vairasyaṁ janayatīti bhāvaḥ | darśayann ity aho sakhāyaḥ adya mṛto’yaṁ sarpo vasāraktādi-kalilo vartata eveti paśyateti tad vadhasya vraje prakhyāpanārthaṁ yoga-māyayaiva tāvat kāla-paryantaṁ tat tad ācchāditam āsīd iti jñeyam vanāt vana-viharaṇāt vrajañ-jagāmeti śeṣaḥ ||46||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tato hasann iti teṣāṁ harṣa-darśanāt varṣe gate’pi bhakṣyāṇām avairasya tasya kṣaṇām avairasyaṁ tasya kṣaṇārdhe pareśāt teṣāṁ rasātmakatvāc ca carmājagaram iti śauṣkasyāpi tasya sadyo mṛtatva-pradarśanaṁ tad vadha-caritasya vraje vyākhyānārthaṁ vanād vana-vihārād vrajaṁ prati nyavartata jagam ety arthaḥ ||46||

...

|| 10.14.47 ||

barha-prasūna-nava-dhātu-vicitritāṅgaḥ |

proddāma-veṇu-dala-śṛṅga-ravotsavāḍhyaḥ ||

vatsān gṛṇann anuga-gīta-pavitra-kīrtir |

gopī-dṛg-utsava-dṛśiḥ praviveśa goṣṭham ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : proddāmo veṇv-ādis tasya ravair utsavas tenāḍhyaḥ saṁpannaḥ | gṛṇann upalālanair āhvayan | gopī-dṛśām utsava-rūpā dṛśir darśanaṁ yasya saḥ |

————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : nava dhātavas tu—haritāla-gairika-hiṅgalumanaḥ śilasaurāṣṭrī-vīra-saubhāgyatutthavolākhyāḥ |

yad vā, nūyante iti navā deva-dvijādayaḥ, tair dhīyante dhāryanta iti nava-dhātūni candanāni, dadhātestuḥ | tāni ca—śveta-raktāgurvādi-bhedenānekāni tair vicitritāṅgaḥ proddāmo mahān | upalālanair auṣṭa-saṁyogaja-śabddādibhiḥ | iti nija-priya-sahacara-bālaka-batsa-saṅgati-janita-harṣabharato vanya-veśādi-viśeṣeṇa vrajastrī-janānāṁ netrānandaṁ sañjanayan vrajāntar-jagāmety āha—varheti | vana dhātur gairikādiḥ | vana-padaṁ gṛha-labhya-suvarṇādi-vyavacchedārtham | prācīna-pustakeṣu tu navadhātuḥ iti pāṭha eva, so’ty arthaikyena samyag eveti barhādibhir viśeṣaṇa-citritāni bhūṣitāny aṅgāni yena | kiṁ vā, sakhibhir yasya | ataḥ proddāmo’tyucco yo veṇudaśa-śṛṅgāṇāṁ ravas tena sa evotsavaḥ kiṁ vā, sa cotsavaś ca nṛtya-krīḍīḍā-gītādi-rūpa tenāḍhyaḥ parma-samṛddhimān | ata evānugais tair eva bālakair gītā harṣabhareṇa gītavat susvaraṁ tālādi-saṁyutm uccaiḥ kīrtitā pavitrā nirmalā jagat pāvanī vā aghāsurādi-vadha-rūpā yasya | gopī-śabdena śrī-yaśodādyāḥ sarvā eva vrajastriyaḥ, tāsām apīdānīm eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-harṣabhareṇādhikaṁ netrānandotpatteḥ ||47||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : iti nija-priya-sahacara-bālaka-vatsa-saṅgati-janita-haryabharato vanya-veṣādi-viśeṣeṇa vrajastha-strī-janānāṁ netrānandaṁ sañjanayan vrajāntar-jagām ety āha—varheti | vana-dhātur-gairikādir-varhādibhir viśeṣeṇa citrāṇi bhūṣitāny aṅgāni yena | kiṁ vā, sakhibhir yasya saḥ | ataḥ proddāmo’tyuco yo veṇu-dala-śṛṅgāṇāṁ ravas tena sa evotsavaḥ | kiṁ vā, utsavaś ca nṛtya-gītādi-rūpas tenāḍho’taevānugais tair eva bālakair gītā harṣabhareṇa gītavat susvaraṁ tālādi-sahitam uccaiḥ kīrtitā pavitrā nirmalā jagat-pāvanī vā kīrtir aghāsura-vadhādi-rūpā yasya | gopī-śabdena śrī-yaśodādayaḥḥ sarvā eva vraja-striyas tāsām apīdānīm eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-darśana-harṣabhareṇādhikanetrānandotpatteḥ | yad vā, śrī-kṛṣṇaika-priyā bhagavatyaḥ śrī-rādhādayas tan netrāṇām eva viśeṣatas tad darśanotsava-siddheḥ | anyat tair vyākhyātam |

yad vā, gopīnāṁ dṛgbhis tat-kartṛka-sākṣād darśanair utsavo yasyās tathābhūtā dṛśi dṛṣṭir yasyetyanyo’nyaṁ sandarśana-sukham uktam | etac ca vrajāntareva voddhavyam—tadānīṁ vatsa-pālane vrajād dūra-caratvenādibirahānutpattyā vrajād bahir agre’nabhigamanāt | tac cāgre go-pālane rasa-viśeṣāvirbhāva-samaye vyaktaṁ vakṣyate ||47||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : iti nija-priya-sahacara-bālaka-vatsa-saṅgati-janita-harṣabharato vanya-veṣādi-viśeṣeṇa vrajastha-strī-janānāṁ netrānandaṁ sañjanayan vrajāntar-jagāmety āha—barheti | vana-dhātur vairikādiḥ vana-padaṁ gṛha-labhya-suvarṇādi-vyavacchedārthaṁ varhādibhir viśeṣeṇa vicitritāni bhūṣitāny aṅgāni yena, kiṁ vā, sakhibhir yasya saḥ, ataḥ proddāmo’ty uccaiḥ yo veṇu-dala-śṛṅgāṇāṁ ravaḥ tena sa eva votsavaḥ kiṁ vā, sa ca utsavaś ca nṛtya-krīḍā-gītādi-rūpas tenāḍhyaḥ parama-samṛddhimān ata evānugais tair eva | bālakeḥ gītā harṣa-bhareṇa gītavat susvaraṁ tālādi-sahitam uccaiḥ kīrtitā pavitrā nirmalā jagat pāvanī vā, kīrtiḥ aghāsura-vadhādi-rūpā yasya | gopī-śabdena yaśodādayaḥ sarvā eva vraja-striyaḥ tāsām apīdānīm eva śrī-kṛṣṇa-harṣabhareṇādhika-netrānandotpatteḥ evaṁ sakhyāṁśena śrī-kṛṣṇāvirbhāvebhyas tebhyo’py eṣām ādhikyaṁ darśitam ||47||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : gopyaḥ śrī-yaśodādayaḥ ||47.48||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : gṛṇan upalālanair āhvayan gopīnāṁ vatsalānāṁ dṛśām utsava-rūpā dṛśi-darśanaṁ yasya saḥ ||47||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : varheti vana-dhātur gairikādiḥ proddāmo’cyucco veṇv-ādi-ravas tenotsavenāḍyaḥ anugais tair bālair gītā paritrā loka-pāvanī kīrtir agha-vadhādi-rūpā yasya sa | vatsān gṛṇānn upalālanair āhvayan | gopīnāṁ śrī-yaśodādīnām | vatsalānāṁ dṛśām utsava-rūpa-dṛśir darśanaṁ yasya ||47||

...

|| 10.14.48 ||

adyānena mahā-vyālo yaśodān-nanda-sūnunā |

hato’vitā vayaṁ cāsmād iti bālā vraje jaguḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : yaśodā-nanda-sūnuneti bahūnām uktiḥ sahaiva darśitā | kecid āhur yaśodā-sūnunā apare nanda-sununeti | yad vā, yaśodāṁ nandayatīti yaśodā-nandaḥ sa cāsau sūnuś ceti tathā tena | asmān mahā-vyālād vayaṁ cāvitā rakṣitāḥ |

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : loke mātā-pitror ubhayor nāma-grahaṇa-pūrvakaṁ putranāmoccāraṇaṁ prāyo na dṛśyata ity asvārasyenāha yad veti | kecid yaśodā-suhṛdaḥ, apare nanda-suhṛdaḥ, pitṛto mātur adhika-snehād-bālānāṁ vṛddhanāmoccāraṇasyāyogyatvād vā mātṛ-kulotpannās tu—yaśodā-sūnur ayam iti vadanti | tad anye nanda-sūnur iti toṣiṇīkārāḥ ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : yaśodāyā ānanda-rūpa-sūnuneti tasya tat-karmaṇā tasya eva viśeṣataḥ santoṣotpatteḥ ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : mātṛ-kulotpannair yaśodā-sūnunā anyair nanda-sūnuteti proktam iti jñeyam | yad vā, ati-santoṣeṇa dvayasyaivāviśeṣataḥ praśaṁsā dvābhyām api tābhyāṁ gokula-kula-bhāgyadyotanāc cā ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : yaśodānandayor bhāgyam ānando yaśo vā yasyāt tathā-bhūtena sūnuneti śakapārthivāditvān madhyama-pada-lopī karmadhārayaḥ tasmān mahā-vyālādvayañcāvitāḥ ||48||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : adyānena mahā-vyālo hataḥ tasmād vayaṁ ca avitā rakṣitā kīdṛśenety āha—yaśodeti | mātṛ-pakṣīyair yaśodā-sūnur iti pitṛ-pakṣīyair nanda-sūnur iti coktenety arthaḥ ||48||

...

|| 10.14.49 ||

rājovāca—

brahman parodbhave kṛṣṇe iyān premā kathaṁ bhavet |

yo’bhūta-pūrva-stokeṣu svodbhaveṣv api kathyatām ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) :

vrajaukasāṁ sva-tokeṣu sneha-vally ābdam anvaham |

śanair niḥsīma vavṛdhe yathā kṛṣṇe tv apūrvavat ||[bhā.pu. 10.13.26]

ity-ādinā sva-tokebhyo’pi para-putre kṛṣṇe premādhikyam uktaṁ tatra pṛcchati—brahmann iti ||49||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : loke hi atiguṇavattamād api para-putrād guṇa-hīne’pi sva-putre premādhikyaṁ dṛśyate, tac cātra viruddham, yataḥ sva-putrebhyo’pi parasya nandasya putre premādhikyam iti katham ? iti sandihānaḥ pṛcchati—he brahman iti | sākṣād-bhagavad-rūpatvāt tvaṁ sarvaṁ jānāsīti sambuddhyāśayaḥ | iyān etat-pramāṇakaḥ | tad āha—yo bhūta- ity-ādi ||49||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : brahman ! sākṣād veda-rūpeti tvaṁ sarvam eva vetsīti bhāvaḥ | iyān etāvān | yo yāvān, parodbhava iti śrī-kṛṣṇe teṣāṁ śrī-yaśodānandana-buddhir eva, na tu bhagavad-buddhir yayā svāpatyebhyo’py adhika-snehaḥ syād iti bhāvaḥ |

nanu, vastu-svabhāvena bhaved iti cet, tarhi tatra kā yuktiḥ ? iti pṛcchati—katham iti | ca api | svodbhaveṣv api pāṭhāntaraṁ spaṣṭam | kathyatāṁ tan nirūpyatām | evaṁ tat-prakārasyaiva praśnaḥ, na tu tad-yogyatāyās tad-arthaṁ putrādi-parityāga-suprasiddheḥ |

yad vā, tatropaviṣṭānāṁ keṣāñcit tapaḥ-parāṇāṁ munīnāṁ putrādiṣv eva snehaṁ dṛṣṭvā śrī-bhagavati tam anālokya tān upahasann iva pṛcchati—brahmann iti | kathaṁ kutaḥ ? anyat samānam ||49||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : nanu, snehas tāvat tridhā dṛśyate—viṣaya-saundaryeṇa, mamatā-viśeṣeṇa, svābhāvika-daihika-sambandha-viśeṣeṇa ca | tatra prathamaṁ prāg-vad eva yāvad vatsapa [bhā.pu. 10.13.19] ity-ādy-uktatvāt, dvitīyaś ca tadvad eva tat-tat-sambandhasyānatirekāt | yaś ca tṛtīyaḥ śrī-pradyumnāgamane tan-mātari śrutaḥ, sa tv atra viparīta eva | tarhi kathaṁ vrajaukasāṁ sva-tokeṣu [bhā.pu. 10.13.26] ity-ādikam uktam ? ity abhipretya viśeṣa-bubhutsayā para-bibodhiṣayā vā pṛcchati—brahmann iti |

śrī-kṛṣṇe śrīdāmādi-nāma-rūpābhyāṁ vyakte, na tu sva-svarūpeṇaiva vyakte tasmin, anyathā tatrāpi pūrva-pakṣaḥ prasajjeta, sa ca purā na kṛta iti yaḥ yāvān atra parodbhavatvaṁ, nandas tv ātmaja utpanne [bhā.pu. 10.5.1] ity ukta-siddhāntānusāreṇaivoktam, anyathā śrīman-nanda-yaśode pratyapyayaṁ pūrva-pakṣaḥ prasajjeta, sa ca purā na kṛta iti |

yad vā, yathā kṛṣṇe tv apūrvavat [bhā.pu. 10.13.26] iti dṛṣṭāntite śrī-kṛṣṇa eva pūrva-pakṣaḥ | etat-pakṣe viṣaya-saundaryādhikyam asty eva, kiṁ tu tato’pi mamatā-deha-sambandhayor ādhikyaṁ vivakṣitaṁ, tac ca siddhānta-viśeṣa-bubhutsayeti jñeyam ||49||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : kṛṣṇe śrīdāmādi-nāma-rūpābhyāṁ vyakte yo yāvān ||49||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) :

vrajaukasāṁ sva-tokeṣu sneha-vally ābdam anvaham |

śanair niḥsīma vavṛdhe yatha kṛṣṇe tv apūrvavat ||[bhā.pu. 10.13.26]

ity-ādinā sva-tokebhyo’pi para-putre kṛṣṇe premādhikyaṁ pūrvaṁ vyañjitaṁ, tatra pṛcchati—brahmann iti | parodbhave nanda-putre svodbhaveṣu sva-sva-putreṣv api yaḥ premā abhūta-pūrvaḥ brahma-mohanāt pūrvaṁ na bhūtaḥ | loke hi khalu atiguṇavattamād api para-putrāt guṇa-hīne’pi sva-putre premādhikyaṁ dṛśyate ity ato loka-viruddhatvād idaṁ pṛcchyate iti bhāvaḥ ||49||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : vrajaukasāṁ sva-tokeṣu [bhā.pu. 10.13.26] ity-ādinā sva-putrebhyo’pi kṛṣṇe premādhikyaṁ pūrvaṁ darśitaṁ, tatra pṛcchati—brahmann iti | parodbhave nanda-putre kṛṣṇe svodbhaveṣu sva-putreṣv api yaḥ premā abhūta-pūrvaḥ virañci-mohanāt pūrvaṁ na bhūtaḥ, sa kathaṁ ? tat kathyatām | loke khalu mahā-guṇād api para-putrāt sva-putreṣv alpeṣv api premādhikyaṁ dṛśyate iti loka-viruddhatvān mamāyaṁ praśna iti ||49||

...

|| 10.14.50 ||

śrī-śuka uvāca—

sarveṣām api bhūtānāṁ nṛpa svātmaiva vallabhaḥ |

itare’patya-vittādyās tad-vallabhatayaiva hi ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : śrī-kṛṣṇasya sākṣād ātmatvāt tasminn ātmīyebhyaḥ premādhikyaṁ yujyata iti vaktuṁ prathamaṁ tāvad ātmanaḥ svataḥ preṣṭhatvam, anyeṣāṁ tu tad-upādhikam iti darśayati pañcabhiḥ—sarveṣām apīti ||50||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : ātmīyebhyaḥ deha-putrādibhyaḥ ātmanas tu kāmāya sarvaṁ priyaṁ bhavati [bṛ.ā.u. 2.4.5] iti śruty-abhiprāyeṇāha—anyeṣām iti | tad-upādhikam ātmopādhikam | pañcabhiḥ sarveṣām ity ārabhya, carācaram ity antaiḥ | paramātmā śrī-kṛṣṇo'sau sarveṣām eva priyād ātmano'py adhika-priyaḥ, kim utātmīyebhyaḥ iti sukhada-viṣayebhyaḥ vaktum ādāv-ātmanas svataḥ preṣṭhatvam anyeṣāṁ tad upādhikam evety āha--sarveṣām iti pañcabhiḥ | ataḥ prathamaṁ svātmeti | deha-dehy avivekenāhantāspada-mātram ucyate, mamatāspade prema-vyavacchedārtha, sva-śabdaś ca pratisvam anubhavāpekṣayā | he nṛpeti | tādṛśasyāsya sva-dharmataḥ prajāpālanam api tathaiveti bhāvaḥ | bhūtānām ity aupacārika-bhedena | hīti tatrānubhava-prāmāṇyaṁ bodhayati ||50||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : paramātma śrī-kṛṣṇo’sau sarveṣām eva priyād ātmano’py adhika-priyaḥ, kim utātmīyebhya iti vaktum ātmanaḥ svataḥ preṣṭhatvam anyeṣāṁ ca tad-upādhikam evety āha—sarveṣām iti pañcabhiḥ |

svasyātmeti samyag jñānānutpattyā pareṣām ātmanā saha bheda-dṛṣṭeḥ | kiṁ vā, svasyātmanaḥ preṣṭhāyāḥ svayam eva sākṣād anubhava-siddheḥ | ādya-śabdād gṛha-dārādayopatyādibhyo’dhika-vallabhasyāpi dehasyānuktir agre dehātma-rāgiṇāṁ mate, viśeṣeṇa dehasyaiva priyatamatāyā vakṣyamāṇatvāt |

yad vā, ādya-śabdena so’pi gṛhītas tasya spaṣṭānuktiḥ sadādhika-pālanādinā tasya vallabhatāyāḥ pratyakṣatvāt | evam agre’pi | he nṛpa ! iti prajā-pālanam api bhavādṛśo rājño yat priyam, tad api ātmātmatvenātmanaḥ priyatvād eveti bhāvaḥ | hi iti tatrānubhavādi-prāmāṇyaṁ bodhayati ||50||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : paramātmā śrī-kṛṣṇo’sau sarveṣām eva priyād ātmano’py adhika-priyaḥ, kim utātmīyebhyaḥ sukhada-viṣayādibhya iti vaktum ādāv ātmanaḥ svataḥ preṣṭhatvam anyeṣāṁ tad-upādhikam evety āha—sarveṣām iti pañcabhiḥ |

atra prathamataḥ svātmā iti deha-dehy-avivekena ahantāspada-mātram ucyate, mamatāspade prema-vyavacchedārtham | sva-śabdaś ca pratisvam anubhavāpekṣayā bhūtānām ity ādāv aupacārika-bhedena | he nṛpa ! iti tvādṛśasya sva-dharmataḥ prajā-pālanam api tatheveti bhāvaḥ | hi iti tatrānubhavādi-prāmāṇyaṁ bodhayati ||50||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : paramātmā śrī-kṛṣṇo’sau sarveṣām eva priyād ātmano’py adhika-priyaḥ kim utātmīyebhyaḥ iti vaktum ādāv ātmanaḥ svataḥ preṣṭhatvam anyeṣāṁ tad-upādhikam evety āha—sarveṣām iti pañcabhiḥ | atra prathamataḥ svātmā iti deha-dehi-vivekena ahantāspada-mātram ucyate | mamatāspade prema-vyavacchedārthaṁ sva-śabdaś ca pratisvam anubhāvāpekṣayā bhūtānām ity aupacārika-bhedena ||50||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : bho rājan ! mamatāspadebhyaḥ putrādibhyaḥ sakāśād ahantāspade ātmani premādhikyam iti loka-rītiḥ prathamaṁ dṛśyatām | tata evāsya siddhānto bhaviṣyatīty āha—sarveṣām iti pañcabhiḥ | vallabhaḥ loka-dṛṣṭyā ātyantika-prīti-viṣayaḥ | sa ca pratideham ekaika eva na tathānye ity āha—itare iti ||50||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : loka-prema-rīti-parīkṣaṇenaivāyaṁ siddhānto bhaved iti tāvad āha—sarveṣām iti | svātmaiva dehendriyādhyakṣo jīva-vallabhaḥ priyaḥ tasya vijñānānandatvād iti bhāvaḥ | tasmād itare’patyādayas tu tad-vallabhatayā tadīyatvenaiva priyā ity arthaḥ ||50||

...

|| 10.14.51 ||

tad rājendra yathā snehaḥ sva-svakātmani dehinām |

na tathā mamatālambi-putra-vitta-gṛhādiṣu ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : kutaḥ ? tathā-darśanād ity āha—tad iti | tasmād eva kāraṇāt sva-svakātmany ahaṅkārāspade dehe ||51||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : kutaḥ sarvebhyaḥ ātmā priyaḥ ? tathā-darśanāt sarvādhika-prema-darśanāt | yata ātmaiva priyaḥ, tasmāt | ahaṅkārāspade ātmatvena svīkṛte, na tv ātmani | yathā mamatāṁ mamatvam ālambanta āśrayanta iti mamatālambīni tāni ca sneha ity anvayaḥ ||51||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tad evāha—tad iti | svārthe kaḥ, svasmin svasminn ātmani dehādhiṣṭhātari, atas tasya sābhimānatvāt tair api vyākhyātam—ahaṅkārāspade iti | ātmani deha iti vyākhyā cāyuktā—tasya nirantara-śloke vakṣyamāṇatvāt | dehināṁ dehātiriktātmajñānavatām ity arthaḥ | mamatālambīty ahantāspadād apy ātmanaḥ sakāśān mamatāspada-puttrādiṣu snehasya nyūnatāyogyatvād ity arthaḥ | he rājendreti sāmrājye'py ātmavat sneho nāstīti bhavatā jñāyata eveti bhāvaḥ ||51||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tad eva vyatirekeṇāha—tad iti | atrāhaṅkārāspada ity antaiva teṣāṁ vyākhyā paratra padye tu ahaṅkārāspade’pi deha ātmety-ādikā jñeyā | pāṭhāntaraṁ tu na saṅgatam, asyaiva vivakṣitatvād dehasya nirantara-śloke vakṣyamāṇatvād mamatālambīti ṣaṣṭhy-arthasya vā vyavahitatvāt nyūnatā-yogyaivety arthaḥ | he rājendra ! iti sāmrājye’py ātmavat snehe nāstīti bhavatā jñāyate eveti bhāvaḥ ||51||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : tad eva vyatirekeṇāha—tad iti ||51.52||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : yathā nirupādhikaḥ ||51||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tat tasmād yathā sva-svakātmani vijñānānande’hantāspade jīvātmani snehas tathā mamatāspadeṣu putrādiṣu tat sambandhiṣu nāsti putra-kalatrayoḥ sva-taulyepi dehādi-pihitatvena svarūpāprakāśāt svato’nadhikatvāc ca na sneho mukhyaḥ ||51||

...

|| 10.14.52 ||

dehātma-vādināṁ puṁsām api rājanya-sattama |

yathā dehaḥ priyatamas tathā na hy anu ye ca tam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : ātmādhyāsa-tāratamyena prema-tāratamyaṁ dṛśyamānaṁ tan-nibandhanam eveti darśayituṁ mūḍhāmūḍha-bhedena viśeṣam āha—dehātma-vādinām iti dvabhyām | taṁ deham anu bhavanti ye putrādayaḥ, te tu na tathā priyatamā ity arthaḥ ||52||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tāratamyaṁ nyūnādhikya-bhāvaḥ | tan-nibandhanam ātma-hetukam | mūḍhāmūḍhau ajña-jñau | rājanyaṁ prāyeṇa dehātma-vādy eva | tvaṁ tu teṣu sattamo’sīti, na tava dehādhyāsa ucita iti sambuddhy-abhiprāyaḥ | ity artha iti—priyaṁ dhanādi, priyataraṁ putrādi, priyatamo deha iti bhāvaḥ | deha evātmetivādinām atyantāvivekinām ity arthaḥ | tan-matepy ātmana eva priya-tamatvaṁ paryavasyet ātmatayaiva dehe'bhimānena priyatamatvāt | hi niścaye | tv atha cakāraḥ | he rājanya-sattameti | kecid rājanyā dehātma-vādino'santa eva, ātmavādinaś ca santaḥ, īśvara-vādinaḥ sattarās teṣu | śrī-kṛṣṇaka-priyatvāt tvaṁ tu sattama iti bhāvaḥ || 52 ||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : deha eva ātmā iti vādinām, atyantāvivekinām ity arthaḥ | ata eva dehātiriktātmājñānād deha eva priyatamas tan-mate’py ātmanaḥ priya-tamatvaṁ paryavasyed eva, ātmatayaiva dehasya mananena priyatamatvāt | hi niścaye | tv-arthe ca-kāraḥ | he rājanya-sattameti kecid rājanyā dehātma-vādino’santa eva, brahma-vādinaś ca santaḥ, īśvara-vādinaḥ sattarāḥ, teṣu śrī-kṛṣṇaika-priyatvāt tvaṁ sattama iti bhāvaḥ ||52||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : deha eva ātmā iti vādinām, atyantāvivekinām ity arthaḥ | tan-mate’py ātmana eva priyatamatvaṁ paryavasyet ātmatayaiva dehābhimānena priyatamatvāt | hi niścaye | tv-arthe ca-kāraḥ | he rājanya-sattama ! iti kecid rājanyā dehātma-vādino’santa eva, ātma-vādinaś ca santaḥ, īśvara-vādinaḥ sattarāḥ, teṣu śrī-kṛṣṇaika-priyatvāt tvaṁ sattama iti bhāvaḥ ||52||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : sa cātmā mūḍhair deha eva jñāyate iti tan-matenāha—deha evātmeti vadituṁ śīlaṁ yeṣāṁ taṁ deham anubhavanti ye putrādayaḥ, te tathā na priyatamā ity arthaḥ ||52||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : mata-bhedenāpy ātmana eva premāspadatvaṁ darśayati—dehātma iti | gauro’haṁ sthūlo’ham iti pratyayāt ye deham evātmānaṁ manyante lokāyatikāḥ, teṣām ātmatvenābhimato deho, yathā priyatamas tathā taṁ deham anu ye'patyādayo bhavanti te na tathā priyā ity ātmaiva vallabha iti ||52||

...

|| 10.14.53 ||

deho’pi mamatā-bhāk cet tarhy asau nātmavat priyaḥ |

yaj jīryaty api dehe’smin jīvitāśā balīyasī ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : yad yasmāj jīryaty api āsanna-maraṇe’pi jīvitāśā bhavati | ayaṁ bhāvaḥ | na jīviṣyatīti niścite'pi dehe yat-premāspadatvaṁ, tad ātma-gatam eva na tu deva-gataṁ, tasya maraṇonmukhatvāt bībhatsatvāc ca | atha vā, tad yasmin dehe jīryaty api jīvitāśā aviveka-daśāyāṁ balīyasyāsītsā tu vivekino yadā mamatā-bhāg-bhavati tadātmavat priyo na bhavatyatas tatra nātīvāstheti ||53||

—————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : ajña-vṛttam uktvā jña-vṛttam āha—ayaṁ bhāva iti | na hi vivekino dehādy-asaj-jaḍe’dhyāso bhavati cin-niṣṭhatvāt tasyeti tātparyam |

nanu, viveka-daśātaḥ prāk tu teṣām api dehādhyāso’sty eveti cet, tatrāha—athaveti | so’pi mamatāśrayo deho’pi | tadā mamatva-samaye’pi priyo na, vināśitva-niścayāt | ato vināśitvād eva | tatra dehe | nātīvāsthā nātyantaṁ sneha ity arthaḥ | mameti dehaṁ manyamānānām avivekināṁ matam ālambyāha—dehopoti | tair vyākhyātam | tatra prathama-pakṣe apīti sambhāvanāyāṁ putrādy apekṣayā samuccaye vā, dvitīya-pakṣe'viveka-daśāyām iti balīyasīti viśeṣaṇenākṣipyate, etat pratiyogitayā vivekina ity api | asau dehopīty anayor anvitayor artha vyācaṣṭe-sopīti | nātīvāsthā iti-mriyatāṁ jīvatu vety apekṣādhikyaṁ nāstīty arthaḥ | idaṁ nātmavat priya ity asya vyākhyānam iti | yad vā, ātmavat pūrvam avivekenātmatayā gṛhīto'hantāviṣayo dehas tad-vat priyo na bhavatīty arthaḥ | yady asmāj jīryati rogādinābhibhūte mamatāspade'smin deha-viṣaye jīvitāśā ayaṁ dehaś ciraṁ tiṣṭhatviti vāñchāpi abalīyasī pūrvāpekṣayā svalpādhi bhavati, vivekato'sminn ātmatāpa-gamenātipriyatvābhāvāt ||53|| atra viśvanāthaḥ

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : mameti dehaṁ manyamānānāṁ vivekināṁ matam ālambyāha—deho’pīti | tair vyākhyātam | tatra nātīvāstheti mriyatāṁ jīvatu vety apekṣādhikaṁ nāstīty arthaḥ iti | yad vā, ātmavat pūrvam avivekenātmatayā gṛhīto’hantā-viṣayo yo dehas tadvan na priyo bhavatīty arthaḥ | yad yasmāt jīryati rogādinābhibhūte’smin mamatāspade dehe viṣaye jīvitāśā ayaṁ dehas tiṣṭhatv ati vāñchāpi abalīyasī pūrvāpekṣayā svalpaiva bhavati vivekato’sminn ātmatvābhimānāpagamenātipriyatvābhāvāt ||53||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : mameti dehaṁ manyamānānām avivekināṁ matam ālambyāha—deho’pīti | tair vyākhyātam | tatra prathama-pakṣe apīti sambhāvanāyāṁ putrādy-apekṣayā samuccaye vā dvitīya-pakṣe aviveka-daśāyām iti balīyasīti viśeṣaṇenākṣipyate etat-pratiyogitayā vivekina ity apy asau deho’pīty anayor anvitayor arthaṁ vyācaṣṭe—so’pīti | nātīvāstheti mriyatāṁ jīvatu vety apekṣādhikyān nāstīty arthaḥ | idaṁ nātmavat priya ity asya vyākhyānam iti | yad vā, ātmavat pūrvam avivekenātmatayā gṛhīto’hantā-viṣayo yo dehas tadvat priyo na bhavatīty arthaḥ | yady asmāj jīryati rogādinābhibhūte’smin mamatāspade deha-viṣaye jīvitāśā ayaṁ dehas tiṣṭhatv iti vāñchāpi abalīyasī pūrvāpekṣayā svalpāpi bhavati, vivekato’sminn ātmatāpa-gamenātipriyatvābhāvāt ||53||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : mameti dehaṁ manvānānāṁ vivekināṁ matam ālambyāha—deho’pīti | atra dehād ahantā-mamatayor apagamenātma-gatam eva saṅgacchata iti ṭīkā yojyā putrādy-apekṣayā samuccayārtho’yam api-śabdaḥ | ṭīkāyāṁ ca aviveka-daśāyām iti balīyastva-viśeṣaṇenākṣipyate etat pratiyogitayā vivekina ity api so’pīty asau deho’pīty anayor vyañjakam iti jñeyam | saralārthas tv ayaṁ yasmān mamatā-bhāktvād eva putrādāv iva dehe jīryati tasya dehasya jīvitāśā balīyasī bhavati mamatā cātma-sambandhād eveti ||53||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : dehātma-vādināṁ teṣām api kadācid īṣad viveke sati ātmaiva priyaḥ syān na tathā deha ity āha—deho’pi ahantāspadībhūto’pi deha | īṣad vivekena yadi mamatā-bhāk syāt tarhy asau deha ātmavat priyo na bhavet, kiṁ tv ātmānurodhenaiva priyaḥ syād ity arthaḥ | tatra lokānubhavam eva pramāṇayati—yad iti | sarvatra deha-tyāge ātmano’tikaṣṭaṁ dṛṣṭvā tad api kaṣṭaṁ mamātmano mā bhavatv iti buddhyaiva ātmany atisnehād eva dehe jīvitāśā adhikā bhavatīty arthaḥ ||53||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : teṣām eva tattvait prasaṅgeneṣad-viveka-lābhe sati deho’pi cen mamatā-bhāk syāt tarhy asāv ātmavat priyo na bhavet, kintu tat-sambandhenaivety arthaḥ | lokānubhavaṁ pramāṇayati—yad iti | yasmād rogādinā dehe jīryati sati tatrātikleśaṁ vīkṣyātmano mama so’tikleśo mā bhūd ity ātmany atisnehād eva dehe jīvitāśā balīyasī bhavatīty arthaḥ ||53||

...

|| 10.14.54 ||

tasmāt priyatamaḥ svātmā sarveṣām eva dehinām |

tad-artham eva sakalaṁ jagac caitac carācaram ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tad-artham eva sakalaṁ priyam ity arthaḥ ||54||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : yata ātmano’nyaḥ ko’pi na priyatamas tasmāt | svātmā priyatamo nirupādhi-premāspadaḥ, prajvalati gṛhe sthitaṁ putrādikam ānetuṁ praveśādi-darśanād iti bhāvaḥ | tad-arthaṁ svātmārtham | ity artha iti—pūrvodāhṛta-śruter iti bhāvaḥ | tasmād iti | caraṁ putra-kalatrādi | acaraṁ gṛha-ghaṭādi | tena loka-dṛṣṭyā putrādibhyaḥ sakāśād ātmana evātyantika-prīti-viṣayatvaṁ pratipāditam ||54||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : caraṁ dehāpatyādi, acaraṁ gehādi tadātmakam etat jagat-prapañcaḥ | ca api sakalam api | yad vā, sakalaṁ deha-daihikaṁ jagac ca carācara-dravyāṇāṁ yajñādi-sādhanatayā tat-tat-phalopabhogena ca kramaśaḥ sarvasyāpy ātmātmatvāt | anyat tair vyākhyātam | yad vā, tad-arthaṁ tat sukhārthaṁ tat sukhārtham evety arthaḥ ||54||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : caraṁ dehāpatyādi, acaraṁ gehādi tadātmakam etaj jagac cāpi sakalam api yat kiñcid ity arthaḥ | etenātmanaḥ sukha-svarūpatvaṁ ca bodhitam ||54||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : evātmanaḥ svataḥ-priyatvena mukhya-svarūpatvaṁ ca bodhitam ||54||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tasmād iti | caraṁ putra-kalatrādi | acaraṁ gṛha-ghaṭa-paṭādi tena loka-dṛṣṭyā putrādibhyaḥ sakāśād ātmana evātyantika-prīti-viṣayatvaṁ pratipāditam ||54||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : nigamayati—tasmād iti | caram apatyādi | acaraṁ gṛhādi sakalaṁ tad-artham eva priyatamam iti loka-dṛṣṭyātmana evātisneha-viṣayatvam upapāditam ||54||

...

|| 10.14.55 ||

kṛṣṇam enam avehi tvam ātmānam akhilātmanām |

jagad-dhitāya so’py atra dehīvābhāti māyayā ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : prastutam āha—kṛṣṇam enam iti ||55||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : yad-artham uktaṁ tad āha—akhilātmanāṁ sarva-jīvānām | nanu, sarvāntaryāmiṇo’dṛśyatvāt kathaṁ dehi-rūpeṇa pratītiḥ ? tatrāha—māyayā kṛpayā | tatra hetuḥ—jagatāṁ nija-darśana-śravaṇavatāṁ hitāya bandha-nivṛttaye | nirguṇa-rūpasya jñātum aśakyatvād bhakta-kṛpayā sa-guṇa-vigraham āviścakāreti bhāvaḥ ||55|| atra viśvanāthaḥ toṣiṇikaroś ca |

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : akhilānām ātmānaṁ kṣetrajñānām ātmānam antaryāmitvāt paramātmānam ity arthaḥ | evaṁ śrī-yaśodānandanaṁ so’pi īdṛśo’pi atrāvatīrṇaḥ loke vā dehīva yathānyo janas tadvad ābhāti īṣat prakāśate | tac ca māyayā tasya tattva-jñānenaiva laukikatve’pi sarvatraivālaukikatvāt tac cādāv uktam eva īṣad iti tattvataḥ prāyaḥ paramaiśvarya-prakaṭanābhiprāyeṇa |

yad vā, māyayā kṛpayā dehī jīva iva san ā samyak bhāti rājate ukta-yuktyā priyatvena sādhitānām akhilātmanām evātmānaṁ jagad-dhitāya kṛṣṇam avehi jagatāṁ duḥkha-dhvaṁsāya paramānanda-prāptaye ca kṛṣṇa-svarūpeṇāvatīrṇam avehīty arthaḥ |

nanu, kṛṣṇe snehaḥ paramātmatayopādhir iti ced, ucyate—tarhi asurāṇām api vastutas tathātvāt teṣāṁ snehaḥ kathaṁ notpadyate ? tatrāha—so’pīti | so’pi dehi-deha-vibhāga-rahito’pi atra eṣu dṛṣṭeṣv asurādiṣu māyayā tadīya-svābhāvika-premāspadatvād vāvaraṇa-śaktyā dehīva prākṛta-śarīrīva ābhāti | tathā coktaṁ mahā-vārāhe—deha-dehi-vibhāgo’tra neśvare vidyate kvacit [bā.rā.pu. 3.5.123] iti |

ayaṁ bhāvaḥ—āvaraṇa-bhāvād eva tat-priyatvānubhāvaḥ syāt mal-loke paramotkaṭa-doṣāvṛtā ātmānam api vyāpādayanto dṛśyante, tad-āvaraṇaṁ tu bhaktyaiva samyag apayāti, tena bhakti-tāratamyenaiva perma-tāratamyam | ato vraja-vāsināṁ sarvātiśāyi-bhajana-viśeṣāt kṛṣṇe premādhikyam eveti ||55||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ deha-dvayātiriktasya śuddhasyātmanaḥ svataḥ priyatvam uktvā vivakṣitam āha—kṛṣṇam iti |

kṛṣir bhū-vācakaḥ śabdo ṇaś ca nirvṛti-vācakaḥ |

tayor aikyaṁ paraṁ brahma kṛṣṇa ity abhidhīyate || [b.s. 5.1]

ity etal-lakṣaṇatvena tan-nāmānam enaṁ śrī-yaśodānandana-rūpam akhilānām ātmānaṁ sūrya-maṇḍala-sthānīyasya tasya raśmi-paramāṇu-sthānīyānāṁ śuddhānām api kṣetrajñānāṁ parama-svarūpatvena paramātmānam avehi |

tarhi kathaṁ loke dṛśyatayā bhāti ? tatrāha—jagad-dhitāya iti | so’pi sarvātma-parama-svarūpa-rūpo’pi parama-kalyāṇa-guṇatvena parama-kāruṇikatvāt sva-bhakta-prasaṅgena jagato’pi hitāyātra jagati bhāti, kalpe kalpe svarūpa-śaktyā prakāśate |

nanu, yadi tādṛśa eva kṛṣṇaḥ, tarhi kathaṁ dehātma-vibhāgādinā tad-viruddha-dharma ivābhāti ? tatrāha—māyayeti | ātmārāmāṇāṁ tat-priya-janānāṁ cātmādhika-nirupādhi-para-premāspada-sarvāṁśatvena tad-vyatirikta-vastu-sambhedābhāvād iti bhāvaḥ | nirupādhi-parama-premāspadatvaṁ khalv ātmatvam ānandatvaṁ ceti | ata eva śrī-madhvācārya-dhṛtaṁ mahā-vārāha-vacanaṁ—deha-dehi-vibhāgo’tra neśvare vidyate kvacit [bā.rā.pu. 3.5.123] iti |

tad evam asurādīnāṁ māyāvaraṇān na tathā bhāti, nāhaṁ prakāśaḥ sarvasya yoga-māyā-samāvṛtaḥ [gītā 7.25] iti śrī-bhagavad-gītāsu ca, tatra yoga-māyā-durghaṭa-ghaṭanā-kāri kim api mama buddhi-sauṣṭhavam iti śrī-svāmi-caraṇāś ca tat-priya-janānāṁ tu tat-prema-bhāvitāntaḥ-karaṇe kṣīre sitotpalavad eka-jātīyatvena premāspadatā-svabhāvato’sau sva-mādhurībhir adhikam ābhāti | anyatra tu yathocitam iti sthite, sarvātiśayi tat-prema-svabhāvānāṁ śrī-vraja-vāsināṁ kim uteti bhāvaḥ ||55||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bhagavat-sandarbhaḥ (44) : enaṁ naumīḍya te’bhra-vapuṣe [bhā.pu. 10.14.1] ity-ādi-varṇita-rūpam avehi mat-prasāda-labdha-vidvattayaivānubhava, na tu tarkādīnāṁ vicārayety arthaḥ | evam-bhūto’pi māyayā kṛpayā jagad-dhitāya sarvasyāpi svātmānaṁ prati cittākarṣaṇāya dehīva jīva ivābhāti krīḍati | iva-śabdena śrī-kṛṣṇas tu jīvavat pṛthag-dehaṁ praviṣṭavān iti gamyate |

ata eva śrī-vigrahasya parama-puruṣārtha-lakṣaṇatvam uktaṁ śrī-dhruveṇa—

satyāśiṣo hi bhagavaṁs tava pāda-padmam

āśīs tathānubhajataḥ puruṣārtha-mūrteḥ [bhā.pu. 4.9.17] ity atra |

ṭīkā ca—he bhagavan puruṣārthaḥ paramānandaḥ sa eva mūrtir yasya tasy ata-eva pāda-padmam āśiṣo rājyādeḥ sakāśāt satyā | āśīḥ paramārtha-phalaṁ hi niścitaṁ kasya tena prakāreṇa tvam eva puruṣārtha ity evaṁ niṣkāmatayā anubhjataḥ | ity eṣā ||55||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : atha vivakṣitam āha—kṛṣṇam iti | evaṁ śrī-yaśodānandana-rūpa atra jagati jagato hitāyābhāti svayaṁ prakāśate dehīva dehātma-vibhāgādinā tad-viruddha-dharma iva māyayaivābhāti na kevalaṁ sarveṣāṁ jīvānām eva parama-svarūpam api tu ante sarveṣāṁ jaḍānām ||55||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : vivakṣitaṁ siddhāntaṁ pratipādayaṁs tattva-dṛṣṭyā tasyāpy ātmana āpekṣika-prīti-viṣayatvam eva ātyantika-prīti-viṣayatvaṁ kevalaṁ kṛṣṇasyaivety āha—kṛṣṇam iti | akhilānām ātmanāṁ jīvānām api ātmānaṁ paramātmānam eva kṛṣṇam avehi, tena putrādiṣu prītir yathā dehānurodhena dehe ca prītir yathā dehānurodhena dehe ca prītir yathā ātmānurodhena tathaivātmany api prītiḥ paramātmānurodhena, sa ca paramātmā kṛṣṇa eva mūrtaḥ pūrṇa eva | yad uktaṁ—viṣṭabhyāham idaṁ kṛtsnam ekāṁśena sthito jagat [gītā 10.42] ity ataḥ kṛṣṇasyaivātyantika-prīti-viṣayatvāt tatraiva prīteḥ parākāṣṭheti sva-putrebhyo’pi tatra yat premādhikyaṁ tad upapāditam |
